Read-only review before a WebAssembly link. Do not modify files. Do not run make, em++, or any build.

Repository: /home/jiajun/LibreOffice/study_LiteCore (sub-project wasm_sdk_probe/). Spec: specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md, section 5.1 (engine) and 6.1 (criteria F1-F3); the spec is in Traditional Chinese.

The change under review is findings/evidence/ms-formats/pre-link/codex/diff.patch (commits 05f311d1f and 4b4d33cac). It lets two new engine profiles open and save Microsoft formats:

- Writer `e2-editor-v33`: accept `.docx` beside `.odt` at `oxsdk_document_open`, give the MEMFS input file the accepted suffix, accept `docx` beside `odt` at `oxsdk_document_save`, report `format` in the `opened` JSON, add capability bits OPEN_DOCX (1<<20) and SAVE_DOCX (1<<21).
- Calc `ods-editor-v5`: the same with `.xlsx`/`xlsx`, bits OPEN_XLSX (1<<22), SAVE_XLSX (1<<23).
- Everything is under a new define `OXSDK_MS_FORMATS`; older builds must preprocess to exactly the tokens they had before.
- The table lives in wasm_sdk_probe/src/document_format.hpp. Workers are derived from the frozen predecessors by tests/v33-worker-delta.json and tests/ods-v5-worker-delta.json (tools/derive_ms_formats_worker.py). Manifests are derived by tools/build_ms_formats_profile.py. Make rules are appended at the end of wasm_sdk_probe/Makefile; tools/link_ms_formats_owner.sh runs both links.

What was already checked (do not repeat, but say if a check is weaker than it claims):
- F1: tests/document_format_test.cpp passes; tests/document_format_mutations.py: 18 generated mutations, all red.
- F2: tools/check_ms_formats_syntax.py, em++ -fsyntax-only of every compile command `make -n -B` prints for both probe.js targets, with and without -DOXSDK_MS_FORMATS, positive controls inside the new blocks of sdk_api.cpp and probe_engine.cpp, and -E "every exported _oxsdk_ name has a body": all green (findings/evidence/ms-formats/pre-link/em-checks/report.json).
- F3: tests/ms_formats_worker_test.cjs passes, and both frozen base workers fail it.

Questions, in order of what would cost us most after a link (each link takes ~3 minutes and mints a new artifact hash):
1. Anything that makes either link fail or the profile refuse to start (capability bits vs `expectedCapabilityBits`, exports, the worker's init checks against the manifest, the Makefile rules, the link script).
2. Any older build whose preprocessed output changes (an edit outside an `#ifdef OXSDK_MS_FORMATS`).
3. Paths where a `.docx`/`.xlsx` name or format is accepted by one layer and refused by another (sdk_api.cpp, probe_engine.cpp, the worker gates, the manifest), or where the open reply's `format` can disagree with the MEMFS suffix.
4. Save: Writer's save path writes `/tmp/oxsdk-output-<handle>-<id>.<format>` and calls LOK `saveAs(url, format)`; anything wrong for `docx`/`xlsx` (e.g. the Calc build's save, the ordered-reply wrapper in the Writer build, a filter-options requirement)?
5. Anything in the worker deltas that changes behaviour for ODF documents.

Answer in English, concisely: a numbered list of findings, each with file:line, why it matters, and a suggested fix; then a short list of things you checked and found fine. If you find nothing for a question, say so.

# SPEC-P-ms-formats：開啟微軟文件

2026-11-02 擁有者的 demo 清單第 3 項原文：

> 開微軟文件（可參考 Collabora）

2026-11-03 18:45 擁有者選它當下一個 session 的工作（`handoff/HANDOFF-2026-11-03g-calc-undo-shipped-0255.md` 最後一節）。

本文件先釘**事實**（第 1～3 節：讀碼、Collabora 的作法、連結前的量測），再列**要擁有者決定的事**（第 4 節）。設計與判準等裁示後 append，判準在實作前釘死。本文件只准 append。

## 1. 現況（讀碼，2026-11-03）

### 1.1 宿主（`/home/jiajun/Projects/Nextcloud_Apps/litecore` @ `5db53ac`，0.2.55）

- `src/files.js`：檔案動作 `litecore-open` 只認 ODT 與 ODS 兩個 mime（`PAGES` 對照表），DOCX／XLSX 在 Files 裡根本不會出現「Open in LiteCore」。
- `lib/Controller/EditorController.php`：頁面表每頁**一個** mime（`e2-editor.html` → ODT，`ods-editor.html`／`ods-viewer.html` → ODS），`resolve()` 對不上就回 `not an ODT file`。
- `js/litecore-editor.js`：`documentFormat()` 由**頁面名稱**推出 mime 與副檔名，存檔的 PUT、衝突副本的檔名（`stem.replace(/\.(?:odt|ods)$/i, '')` 加 ODF 副檔名）都用它。
- 新增選單（0.2.54）只建 ODT／ODS，與本項無關。

### 1.2 頁面

- 有宿主時，頁面把 `HOST.load()` 給的檔名原樣交給引擎（Writer `openFromHost()`，Calc 第 638 行）。
- 沒有宿主時（鏡像）：檔案選擇器 `accept` 只有 `.odt`／`.ods`；Writer 下載檔名固定 `-v2.odt`，Calc 的 Blob 型別固定 ODS。
- Calc session 存檔固定 `format: "ods"`（`spreadsheet-shell/calc-editor-session.js:378`）；SDK 的預設是 `"odt"`（`sdk/document-sdk.js:772`）。

### 1.3 引擎（我們自己的程式碼）

- **開檔**：`oxsdk_document_open`（`src/sdk_api.cpp:117-141`）在進佇列之前就拒絕檔名——Writer 只收 `.odt`，Calc 的 profile 只收 `.odt`／`.ods`（`src/ods_viewer.hpp` 的 `acceptedSuffix`）。Writer 寫進 MEMFS 的輸入檔名固定 `.odt`（`src/probe_engine.cpp` 的 `writeInputFile`）。
- **存檔**：`oxsdk_document_save` 只收 `odt`（Writer）或 `ods`（Calc），之後 `saveAs(url, format)`；LOK 把 `docx` 對到 `Office Open XML Text`、`xlsx` 對到 `Calc Office Open XML`、`doc`／`xls` 對到 `MS Word 97`／`MS Excel 97`（`libreoffice-26-8/desktop/source/lib/init.cxx:298-334`）。
- 這就是 **finding 013**（2026-08-02，至今未關）：同一份合法 DOCX，名字叫 `.docx` 在 C ABI 前被拒，名字假裝 `.odt` 卻能開。當時的決定：**不准用假檔名宣稱支援**，要支援就做窄的 `format` 介面，讓驗證、MEMFS 路徑、manifest capability 三者一致。本文件沿用這個決定。

### 1.4 引擎裡**已經有**的（不用重編 core）

- 兩顆出貨的 wasm 都帶著 `com_sun_star_comp_Writer_WriterFilter_get_implementation`（DOCX／RTF 匯入）、`..._DocxExport_...`、`..._RtfFilter_...`、`com_sun_star_comp_oox_FormatDetector_...`；Calc 的 `ods-editor-v4` 另有 `com_sun_star_comp_oox_xls_ExcelFilter_...`（XLSX 匯入匯出）與 `..._ExcelBiffFormatDetector_...`。三棵 core 樹的 `soffice.js.linkdeps` 都有 `-lmswordlo -lsw_writerfilterlo -looxlo`，Calc 的另有 `-lscfiltlo -lscdlo`。
- 兩顆的檔案映像（`base-r5-no35`、`base-viewer-v3-no35`）的 registry 都有 `MS Word 2007 XML`、`Office Open XML Text`、`MS Word 97`、`Calc MS Excel 2007 XML`、`MS Excel 97` 的類型與濾鏡定義。
- 所以要改的只有**我們自己的引擎程式碼**（開檔、存檔的允許清單與 capability），要**連結**（Writer 一次、Calc 一次），不用重編 core。

## 2. Collabora 的作法（讀碼，2026-11-03）

來源：`cool-26-04`（COOL co-26.04）、`/home/jiajun/OSSII/ncdev_env/apps/richdocuments`（richdocuments 31.1.1，OSSII 的 fork；fork 自己改的地方另外標出）。逐條出處在 `findings/evidence/ms-formats/collabora-reading.md`。

- **哪些格式能編輯**：discovery 裡 doc、docx、docm、dotx、rtf、xls、xlsx、xlsb、xlsm、xltx、csv、ppt、pptx 都有 `edit`；dot、pot 只有 `view`。不在 `edit` 清單裡的副檔名一律唯讀。
- **存回什麼格式**：**原格式**。一般存檔與自動存檔都是 `.uno:Save`，不帶 FilterName，沿用文件開啟時的濾鏡，再用 WOPI PUT 寫回**同一個檔案**。只有使用者自己按「另存新檔」才會換格式。
- **格式警告**：COOL 前端只對 txt、csv、xlsb 一開始唯讀並提示「可能有內容無法以目前格式儲存，要不要存成 ODF」；**docx／xlsx／doc／xls 沒有任何警告**。core 的「保留目前格式」對話框在 LOK 下會不會跳出來，讀碼沒有定論。
- **舊式二進位格式（.doc／.xls）**：開檔不轉換，照樣編輯、存回原格式。
- **新增選單**：預設 ODF；管理員勾選 `doc_format = ooxml` 才改成建 docx／xlsx／pptx。
- **OSSII fork 自己加的**：`saveToOdf` 設定（預設 `yes`，管理介面叫「Automatically convert Microsoft Office files to ODF files」），在 CheckFileInfo 送出 `SaveToOdf = odt|ods|odp`。**原版 COOL 不讀這個欄位**，要配 OSSII 自己的前端才會生效。
- 密碼文件：開檔時詢問密碼；巨集：預設不執行。

## 3. 連結之前的量測（2026-11-03）

證據 `findings/evidence/ms-formats/`（英文 README）。兩支工具：

- **M0 原生**：`wasm_sdk_probe/tools/msformat_native.cpp`（LOK，原生 core 26.8 `build-native-26-8`，單檔 g++ 3 秒）與驅動 `tools/run_msformat_native.py`。每份檔案兩臂：真檔名、改成 `.odt`／`.ods`（現行引擎能接受的名字）。真檔名臂存三次——LOK `saveAs(原格式)`（引擎的存法）、用**匯入時的濾鏡** `storeToURL`（Collabora `.uno:Save` 的存法）、ODF——每份輸出再開一次。內容比對：Writer 依序的段落字串與表格儲存格，Calc 每張表每個非空格的 `getFormula()`。
- **M1 WASM**：`tools/run_msformat_wasm.py`，**現行出貨**的頁面與凍結 profile（Writer `e2-editor-v32`、Calc `ods-editor-v4`），不連結。因為 finding 013，每份都用 ODF 檔名交給頁面（**量測，不是產品路徑**）。透過頁面自己的宿主介面（`globalThis.liteCoreHost`，假宿主由 CDP 在頁面載入前放進去），按頁面的存檔鈕，把存出的 ODF 交給 M0 的原生 harness 讀，再和 M0 的「原生 ODF 來回」逐行比。只有 Chrome（Firefox 的 WebDriver 沒有載入前注入）。

語料 33 份（清單在 `run_msformat_native.py`，sha256 在報告裡）：LibreOffice QA 樹中 `docProps/app.xml` 寫著 Microsoft 的 7 份 DOCX、10 份 XLSX（表格、頁首、清單、圖、修訂、中日文；多工作表、公式、合併格、圖表、條件格式，一份 24 張表），QA 樹的 3 份 DOC、3 份 XLS，R7 留下的 10 份 DOCX（含一份截斷的負向對照）。

### 3.1 M0 原生的結果

- **開檔**：32／33；截斷的那份照預期失敗。開檔 28–241 ms。
- **類型偵測**：DOCX 15 份 `Office Open XML Text`、1 份 `MS Word 2007 XML`；XLSX 8 份 `Calc Office Open XML`、1 份 `Calc MS Excel 2007 XML`（第 10 份見下）；DOC `MS Word 97`；XLS `MS Excel 97`。
- **改成 ODF 檔名**：DOCX、XLSX、DOC 選到同一個濾鏡，內容相同；**XLS 選到範本變體** `MS Excel 97 Vorlage/Template`（內容相同）。所以 M1 對 DOCX、XLSX 有代表性。
- **存回原格式再開**：內容相同 30／32。不同的兩份：`n830205.docx` 多 2 個空段落（LOK `saveAs` 與沿用匯入濾鏡兩種存法一樣，位元組數相同——**濾鏡選擇不是原因**，引擎直接用 LOK 的 `saveAs` 即可）；`XlStartupExternal.xls` 的 54 格外部增益集公式變成 `=NA()`。Writer 頁數 16／16 不變。存 DOCX 4–37 ms、XLSX 11–269 ms。
- **存成 ODF 再開**：內容相同 29／31；不同的是公式參照大小寫（`a3:m25` → `A3:M25`，計算不變）與 100 格日期時間值的第 16 位數字。但**版面**：`n830205.docx` 從 7 頁變 11 頁；4 份 XLSX 的列高總和變了。存 ODT 91–859 ms、ODS 109–726 ms。
- **一份 XLSX 存成 ODS 後重開不會結束**：`forum-mso-en4-134670.xlsx` 匯入、存 XLSX、存 ODS 都在幾秒內完成，但重開那份 ODS，原生 LOK 跑滿 25 分鐘被砍；主執行緒在 `ScXMLImport::endDocument` → 列高 → `ScInterpreter::ScCountIf`，整欄範圍的 `COUNTIFS` 被逐格重算。桌面版 26.8.1.1 同一份檔也超過 14 分鐘、CPU 100%。上游的效能斷崖，記為 finding 184。

### 3.2 M1 WASM 的結果（現行出貨的引擎，不連結）

- 可開的 32 份**全部**打開，從導覽完成到就緒約 1.2–5.3 秒；截斷的 DOCX 進 `stopped`，通知列是技術字串 `LOK_ERROR：loadComponentFromURL returned an empty reference`。
- 存出的 ODF 與原生 ODF 來回**逐行相同**：可比的 31 份全部（DOCX、XLSX、DOC、XLS 都有）。`forum-mso-en4-134670.xlsx` 在 WASM 上 5 秒開好、存檔正常，比不了是因為讀它存出的 ODS 會撞上 finding 184。工具第一次跑時，取回內嵌字型那份 12.5 MB 的存檔撞到 CDP 1 MiB 上限（是工具的問題，頁面本身已經存好）；改成分段取回後，正式證據裡那份也相同。
- **結論**：出貨引擎的匯入不用改，濾鏡、類型偵測、執行緒都沒有問題。要改的是第 1.3 節的兩道邊界。**DOCX／XLSX 存回原格式在 WASM 上還沒量過**（存檔允許清單擋住），連結後才能量。

## 4. 擁有者裁示（2026-11-03）

1. **存檔格式：存回原格式**（DOCX 存回 DOCX、XLSX 存回 XLSX，寫回同一個檔，和 Collabora 相同）。**另外新增「另存新檔」，可以轉成 ODF。**
2. **範圍：DOCX＋XLSX。** DOC、XLS、docm、xlsm、rtf、csv、範本檔不在這一批。

## 5. 設計（2026-11-03，裁示之後、實作之前）

### 5.1 引擎（要連結：Writer 一次、Calc 一次；core 不動）

新 profile：Writer `e2-editor-v33`（接 v32），Calc `ods-editor-v5`（接 v4）。改動全部在新定義 `OXSDK_MS_FORMATS` 之下，只進這兩顆新 build。

- **E1 一份格式表**：新標頭 `src/document_format.hpp`，取代 `ods_viewer.hpp` 的 `acceptedSuffix` 在新 build 裡的角色（舊 build 的位元組不動）。封閉清單，大小寫不拘：

  | profile | 開檔接受的副檔名 | 存檔接受的格式 |
  |---|---|---|
  | Writer v33 | `.odt`、`.docx` | `odt`、`docx` |
  | Calc v5 | `.odt`、`.ods`、`.xlsx`（`.odt` 是 v4 既有行為） | `ods`、`xlsx` |

  其餘一律在進佇列前回 `INVALID_ARGUMENT`，`.doc`、`.xls`、`.docm`、`.xlsm`、`.dotx`、`.xltx`、`.rtf`、`.csv` 都在拒絕之列。
- **E2 MEMFS 輸入檔用接受時的副檔名**（Writer 目前固定 `.odt`）。M0 證實副檔名不影響 DOCX／XLSX 的濾鏡，但檔名要誠實，這是 finding 013 的處方。
- **E3 存檔**照舊 `saveAs(url, format)`；`docx`、`xlsx` 由 LOK 對到 `Office Open XML Text`、`Calc Office Open XML`。M0 量過，沿用匯入濾鏡的存法沒有差別。
- **E4 capability**：新位元 `OPEN_DOCX`、`SAVE_DOCX`（Writer），`OPEN_XLSX`、`SAVE_XLSX`（Calc）；manifest 對應 `open-docx`、`save-docx`、`open-xlsx`、`save-xlsx`。worker 的閘門：`save` 帶 `docx` 要 `save-docx`，帶 `xlsx` 要 `save-xlsx`（照 v4 worker 判斷 `save-ods` 的寫法）。
- **E5 開檔回覆帶 `format`**：接受時的副檔名（`odt`／`docx`／`ods`／`xlsx`）。頁面與 session 用它決定存檔格式，不各自從檔名再猜一次。

### 5.2 頁面（Writer 第 89 代殼層；Calc 頁面）

- **存檔用文件自己的格式**：`format` 來自 E5。Writer 工具列「儲存 ODT」改成「儲存」；沒有宿主時的下載檔名保留原副檔名。Calc 的 session 不再寫死 `"ods"`。自動存檔同樣存原格式。
- **另存新檔**：兩個頁面的工具列各加一顆「另存新檔」。按下去是頁面自己的對話框：
  - 檔名欄，預設是原檔名去掉副檔名；
  - 格式：**ODF（.odt／.ods）**，文件本身是 DOCX／XLSX 時另有「原格式」。微軟文件預設選 ODF（擁有者的用途是轉成 ODF）。ODF 文件只有 ODF 一項（等於存一份副本）；ODT 另存成 DOCX 不在這一批。
  - 確定後：引擎用選定的格式存一次，交給 `HOST.saveAs(bytes, { name, format })`。宿主說名字已被占用時，對話框留著並說明，不覆寫。沒有宿主時（鏡像）改成下載。
  - 唯讀文件不提供另存新檔（限制 `L-saveas-readonly`）。
- 檔案選擇器（鏡像）的 `accept` 加上 `.docx`／`.xlsx`。

### 5.3 宿主（0.2.56）

- `src/files.js`：DOCX mime（`application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document`）→ `e2-editor.html`；XLSX mime（`application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet`）→ `ods-editor.html`。**唯讀的 XLSX 也走 `ods-editor.html`**，由 `load()` 的 `readOnly` 開成唯讀，因為檢視器 profile（`ods-viewer-v3`）不收 `.xlsx`，不為它另外連結。
- `EditorController`：每頁從「一個 mime」改成「一組 mime」；`ods-editor.html` 不可寫時轉去檢視器，只對 ODS 這麼做。
- `js/litecore-editor.js`：`documentFormat()` 改看檔案的 mime，不看頁面名稱；PUT 的 `Content-Type`、衝突副本的副檔名都跟著檔案。
- **`saveAs(bytes, { name, format })`**：在原檔同一個資料夾 PUT 新檔，`If-None-Match: *`；412 回 `NAME_TAKEN`，不寫。成功之後：放掉原檔的鎖，**改開新檔**（導向新檔的編輯器路由，`fileId` 取自 PUT 回應的 `OC-FileId`，沒有就 PROPFIND），這次離開頁面不詢問。原檔停在最後一次存檔的樣子。這和 richdocuments 另存新檔之後載入新檔的行為相同；好處是鎖、標題、網址、之後存檔的格式都走既有的開檔路徑，畫面上看到的就是新檔真正的內容（例如 7 頁變 11 頁會當場看到），代價是另存之後的復原紀錄不會留著。

## 6. 判準（實作前釘死）

**每一條都附「如果這件事永遠沒做」的預設結論：該項判 NOT_ESTABLISHED，不判過。**

### 6.1 連結之前（原生與靜態）

- **F1** `tests/document_format_test.cpp`（原生，不連 core）：表 E1 的每一格（接受與拒絕，含大小寫、只有副檔名的名字、比副檔名短的名字）。紅案：`tests/document_format_mutations.py` 逐條改表，每個突變都要有測試轉紅；突變清單由工具從表本身產生，不手列。
- **F2** 照 2026-10-28 的規則跑 `-fsyntax-only`（附正向對照）與 `-E` 的「匯出都有本體」檢查，兩顆 build 都跑。
- **F3** worker：`save` 帶 `docx`／`xlsx` 而 manifest 沒有對應 capability 時回 `UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION`；有的時候放行。node 測試，紅案是把閘門拿掉。

### 6.2 連結之後（鏡像，Chrome 與 Firefox，真檔名，經頁面自己的檔案選擇器與存檔鈕）

語料＝第 3 節 M0 清單裡的 DOCX 與 XLSX（工具自 `run_msformat_native.corpus()` 取得，不手列）。

- **W1 存回原格式**：每一份都打開；頁面存出的位元組是 OOXML（zip 裡有 `[Content_Types].xml`、沒有 `mimetype`）；用 M0 的原生 harness 讀它，內容與 M0 同一份檔的「原生原格式來回」相同。截斷的 DOCX 例外：要得到有型別的錯誤，頁面不卡住。
- **W2 另存成 ODF**：每一份另存出的位元組是 ODF（第一個成員是 `mimetype`，值對）；內容與 M0 的「原生 ODF 來回」相同。`forum-mso-en4-134670.xlsx` 因 finding 184 判 NOT_ESTABLISHED，不判過也不判紅。
- **W3 名字邊界**：`.doc`、`.xls`、`.docm`、`.xlsm`、`.rtf` 名字的檔案在 C ABI 前被拒（`INVALID_ARGUMENT`），worker 仍健康、下一次開正常檔成功。
- **W4 回歸**：兩個新 profile 上既有的 ODT／ODS 回歸網照舊全過（Writer 第 89 代的殼層檢查、Calc 的 cell、range、autosave、P2、C、CU）。
- **紅案**：W1、W2 在 v32／ods-editor-v4 上要紅（真檔名在 C ABI 被拒）。

### 6.3 宿主（`host_batch8.py`，Chrome 與 Firefox，擁有者的路：Files、真指標與按鍵、WebDAV 讀回）

- **H8/action**：Files 對 `.docx`、`.xlsx` 有「Open in LiteCore」並是預設動作，分別進 Writer、Calc 編輯器；`.odt`、`.ods` 照舊。
- **H8/save-docx**、**H8/save-xlsx**：打字（Calc 輸入一格）、存檔；WebDAV 讀回是 OOXML、含剛輸入的內容；檔名與 mime 不變；資料夾裡沒有多出別的檔案。
- **H8/autosave-xlsx**：Calc 自動存檔寫回的是 XLSX。
- **H8/save-as-odf**（Writer、Calc 各一）：另存新檔選 ODF；資料夾多出 `名稱.odt`／`.ods`，是 ODF、含編輯內容；原檔的 sha256 等於它最後一次存檔；頁面改開新檔（網址的 `fileId` 是新檔、標題是新檔名）；再存一次寫的是新檔。
- **H8/save-as-taken**：另存成已存在的名字，那個檔的 sha256 不變，對話框說明名字被占用。
- **H8/read-only**：唯讀分享的 DOCX、XLSX 開成唯讀，不取鎖、不能存、沒有另存新檔。
- **紅案**：同一支工具在 0.2.55 上要紅在 H8/action。
- **回歸**：Files 工作流 52/52、`H7_HOLDS`、T6、`HOST_BATCH_HOLDS`、`LAYOUT_HOLDS`。

## 7. 具名限制

- `L-ms-export-loss`：存回原格式有 LibreOffice 匯出本身的損失（M0：1／16 份 DOCX 多空段落）。Collabora 相同。同一個 session 裡重複存檔不會累積損失（每次都從記憶體的模型匯出，不重新匯入）；關掉再開就會再經過一次匯入。
- `L-no-format-warning`：存回微軟格式不提示「可能有內容遺失」，和 Collabora 相同。
- `L-ods-reload-cliff`：finding 184。整欄範圍 `COUNTIFS` 很多的 ODS 開不起來；另存成 ODS 會把這類 XLSX 變成開不起來的檔案。上游問題，不在本批修。
- `L-legacy-formats`：DOC、XLS、docm、xlsm、rtf、csv、範本檔不支援（M0／M1 量到它們打得開，但不在裁示範圍）。
- `L-saveas-ms-from-odf`：ODT／ODS 不能另存成 DOCX／XLSX。
- `L-saveas-readonly`：唯讀文件沒有另存新檔。
- `L-mislabeled`：副檔名與內容不符的檔案照內容開，照副檔名存（例如內容是 ODT、名字叫 `.docx`，存檔後就是真的 DOCX）。
- `L-corrupt-message`：壞掉的微軟文件，通知列是技術字串（`LOK_ERROR：…`），和壞掉的 ODT 相同。

## 8. 如果永遠沒做

Files 對微軟文件沒有「Open in LiteCore」；要先在別處轉成 ODF 再上傳。同事 demo 時只能開 ODT／ODS。

+#ifndef WASM_SDK_PROBE_DOCUMENT_FORMAT_HPP
+#define WASM_SDK_PROBE_DOCUMENT_FORMAT_HPP
+
+// specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 5.1 E1: which names `oxsdk_document_open` accepts and which formats
+// `oxsdk_document_save` writes, per profile, as one closed table.  Only the OXSDK_MS_FORMATS builds
+// (Writer e2-editor-v33, Calc ods-editor-v5) include it; the older builds keep src/ods_viewer.hpp's
+// acceptedSuffix and the literal checks in src/sdk_api.cpp, byte for byte.
+//
+// Held by tests/document_format_test.cpp (native, no core); its red cases, one per table entry and
+// one per rule, are generated from this file by tests/document_format_mutations.py.
+//
+// finding 013's prescription: the name that passes validation is the name the input file gets in
+// MEMFS, and the format the open reply reports, so validation, path and capability say one thing.
+
+#include <cctype>
+#include <cstddef>
+#include <string>
+
+namespace probe::docformat {
+
+enum class Profile { Writer, Calc };
+
+struct Entry {
+  const char *suffix; // accepted at open, lower-case, with the dot
+  const char *format; // reported by the open reply and accepted by save
+  bool savable;       // the save boundary writes this format
+};
+
+// The table.  Calc keeps `.odt` at open because ods-editor-v4 accepts it (ods_viewer.hpp); it was
+// never savable there, and is not here.
+inline constexpr Entry kWriter[] = {
+    {".odt", "odt", true},
+    {".docx", "docx", true},
+};
+inline constexpr Entry kCalc[] = {
+    {".odt", "odt", false},
+    {".ods", "ods", true},
+    {".xlsx", "xlsx", true},
+};
+
+// The profile this build is: the Calc editor's rules define OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR, the Writer editor's do not.
+#ifdef OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR
+inline constexpr Profile kThisBuild = Profile::Calc;
+#else
+inline constexpr Profile kThisBuild = Profile::Writer;
+#endif
+
+template <std::size_t N>
+constexpr std::size_t countOf(const Entry (&)[N]) { return N; }
+
+inline const Entry *table(Profile profile, std::size_t &count) {
+  if (profile == Profile::Calc) {
+    count = countOf(kCalc);
+    return kCalc;
+  }
+  count = countOf(kWriter);
+  return kWriter;
+}
+
+inline std::string lower(std::string text) {
+  for (char &c : text)
+    c = static_cast<char>(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
+  return text;
+}
+
+// The table entry a name is accepted under, or nullptr.  Case does not matter (`REPORT.DOCX` is the
+// same file); a name must have at least one character before the suffix.
+inline const Entry *acceptedEntry(Profile profile, const std::string &name) {
+  std::size_t count = 0;
+  const Entry *entries = table(profile, count);
+  const std::string lowered = lower(name);
+  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+    const std::string suffix = entries[i].suffix;
+    if (lowered.size() > suffix.size() &&
+        lowered.compare(lowered.size() - suffix.size(), suffix.size(), suffix) == 0)
+      return &entries[i];
+  }
+  return nullptr;
+}
+
+// The suffix a name is accepted under ("" for a refusal), as ods_viewer.hpp's acceptedSuffix.
+inline std::string acceptedSuffix(Profile profile, const std::string &name) {
+  const Entry *entry = acceptedEntry(profile, name);
+  return entry ? entry->suffix : "";
+}
+
+// The format the open reply reports for an accepted name ("" for a refusal).
+inline std::string openFormat(Profile profile, const std::string &name) {
+  const Entry *entry = acceptedEntry(profile, name);
+  return entry ? entry->format : "";
+}
+
+// Whether the save boundary writes `format` (compared as written: the worker sends lower case).
+inline bool savable(Profile profile, const std::string &format) {
+  std::size_t count = 0;
+  const Entry *entries = table(profile, count);
+  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i)
+    if (entries[i].savable && format == entries[i].format)
+      return true;
+  return false;
+}
+
+} // namespace probe::docformat
+
+#endif

+++ b/wasm_sdk_probe/src/probe_engine.cpp
@@ -79,6 +79,9 @@

 #endif
 #include "sdk_api.h"
+#ifdef OXSDK_MS_FORMATS
+#include "document_format.hpp"

@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Assemble the Microsoft-formats successors (specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 5.1) without overwriting a frozen profile.  Derived from
+build_calc_undo_profile.py.
+
+Closed list of differences from the frozen base manifest:
+
+  writer  base dist/profiles/e2-editor-v32  -> e2-editor-v33: capabilities + open-docx, save-docx; bits 20, 21
+  calc    base dist/profiles/ods-editor-v4  -> ods-editor-v5: capabilities + open-xlsx, save-xlsx; bits 22, 23
+
+plus `profile`, `sdkVersion` and a `documentFormatContract` naming the table (src/document_format.hpp).  The export list must equal
+the base's (no new export), and the base must not already claim either capability.  --development assembles under build/ only;
+without it the output must not already hold a manifest (a frozen profile is never overwritten).
+  build_ms_formats_profile.py --profile writer|calc --loader F --wasm F --worker F --exports F --base-exports F --output DIR [--development]
+"""
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+import shutil
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+PROFILES = {
+    "writer": {"base": HERE / "dist/profiles/e2-editor-v32", "profile": "e2-editor-v33", "ms": "docx",
+               "bits": (1 << 20) | (1 << 21),  # src/sdk_api.h OXSDK_CAPABILITY_OPEN_DOCX, SAVE_DOCX
+               "open": [".odt", ".docx"], "save": ["odt", "docx"]},
+    "calc": {"base": HERE / "dist/profiles/ods-editor-v4", "profile": "ods-editor-v5", "ms": "xlsx",
+             "bits": (1 << 22) | (1 << 23),  # OXSDK_CAPABILITY_OPEN_XLSX, SAVE_XLSX
+             "open": [".odt", ".ods", ".xlsx"], "save": ["ods", "xlsx"]},
+}
+
+
+def main():
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
+    parser.add_argument("--profile", choices=sorted(PROFILES), required=True)
+    for name in ("loader", "wasm", "worker", "exports", "base-exports", "output"):
+        parser.add_argument("--" + name, type=Path, required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--development", action="store_true")
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    spec = PROFILES[args.profile]
+    base = spec["base"]
+    output = args.output.resolve()
+    if args.development and not output.is_relative_to((HERE / "build").resolve()):
+        raise SystemExit("development assembly must stay under build; frozen profiles are immutable")
+    if (output / "sdk-manifest.json").exists() and not args.development:
+        raise SystemExit("profile already exists; a frozen profile is not overwritten")
+    if output == base.resolve():
+        raise SystemExit("the predecessor profile must never be overwritten")
+    exports, base_exports = set(args.exports.read_text().split()), set(args.base_exports.read_text().split())
+    if exports != base_exports:
+        raise SystemExit(f"the export list moved: added {sorted(exports - base_exports)}, removed {sorted(base_exports - exports)}")
+    manifest = json.loads((base / "sdk-manifest.json").read_text())
+    ms = spec["ms"]
+    if {f"open-{ms}", f"save-{ms}"} & set(manifest["capabilities"]) or manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"] & spec["bits"]:
+        raise SystemExit(f"the base already claims {ms}")
+    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    for name, source in (("probe.js", args.loader), ("probe.wasm", args.wasm), ("sdk-worker.js", args.worker)):
+        shutil.copyfile(source, output / name)
+    for key in ("soffice.data", "soffice.data.js.metadata"):
+        relative = manifest["artifactFiles"][key]
+        if relative.startswith("./"):  # Calc carries its image beside the engine; Writer's lives in ../resources
+            shutil.copyfile(base / relative[2:], output / relative[2:])
+    manifest["profile"] = spec["profile"]
+    manifest["sdkVersion"] = "0.6.0-r6-worker+" + spec["profile"]
+    manifest["capabilities"] += [f"open-{ms}", f"save-{ms}"]
+    manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"] |= spec["bits"]
+    manifest["documentFormatContract"] = {
+        "version": 1, "table": "src/document_format.hpp", "open": spec["open"], "save": spec["save"],
+        "openReply": "format: the accepted suffix's format", "refused": "INVALID_ARGUMENT before the queue",
+    }
+    (output / "sdk-manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + "\n")
+    print(json.dumps({"profile": spec["profile"], "development": args.development,
+                      "capabilityBits": manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"],
+                      "sha256": {name: hashlib.sha256((output / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+                                 for name in ("probe.js", "probe.wasm", "sdk-worker.js", "sdk-manifest.json")}}, indent=2))
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

@@ -0,0 +1,51 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""The Microsoft-formats workers (specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 5.1 E4, E5): a frozen worker plus a closed list of replacements, each
+occurring exactly once, written to --out.
+
+  writer  dist/profiles/e2-editor-v32/sdk-worker.js + tests/v33-worker-delta.json   -> the e2-editor-v33 worker
+  calc    dist/profiles/ods-editor-v4/sdk-worker.js + tests/ods-v5-worker-delta.json -> the ods-editor-v5 worker
+
+Derived at build time instead of living in sdk/, as derive_ods_v4_worker.py: the Writer shell bundle scans sdk/*.js, and a new file
+there turns generation 88's bundle check red.  tests/ms_formats_worker_test.cjs exercises the same derivation.
+  derive_ms_formats_worker.py --profile writer|calc --out FILE
+"""
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SDK = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+BASES = {
+    "writer": (SDK / "dist/profiles/e2-editor-v32/sdk-worker.js",
+               "c394302c589baff3b40ff67a7579e07cf53fc7447dd3f04b26ff9c827280ff36", SDK / "tests/v33-worker-delta.json"),
+    "calc": (SDK / "dist/profiles/ods-editor-v4/sdk-worker.js",
+             "4a06fbe45c458b4c9aa60ab7bcd624894dace729a2b83f009f3e9008ccc8919c", SDK / "tests/ods-v5-worker-delta.json"),
+}
+
+
+def derive(profile):
+    base, sha, delta = BASES[profile]
+    data = base.read_bytes()
+    if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != sha:
+        raise SystemExit(f"the frozen {profile} base worker moved: {base}")
+    text = data.decode("utf-8")
+    for hunk in json.loads(delta.read_text())["hunks"]:
+        if text.count(hunk["old"]) != 1:
+            raise SystemExit(f"hunk {hunk['item']!r} occurs {text.count(hunk['old'])} times")
+        text = text.replace(hunk["old"], hunk["new"])
+    return text
+
+
+def main():
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
+    parser.add_argument("--profile", choices=sorted(BASES), required=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    text = derive(args.profile)
+    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    args.out.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    print(json.dumps({"profile": args.profile, "out": str(args.out), "sha256": hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()}))
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Owner-run product links (specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 5.1): e2-editor-v33 (DOCX) then ods-editor-v5 (XLSX), development
+# profiles under build/ (freezing into dist/ is a later step, after the criteria hold).  Agents may syntax-check this
+# file, never execute it unless the owner asks.  About 3 minutes per link.
+set -euo pipefail
+cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.."
+
+writer_log=build/link-ms-formats-writer-01.log
+calc_log=build/link-ms-formats-calc-01.log
+for path in "$writer_log" "$calc_log" build/e2/editor-v33/probe.js build/ods/editor-v5/probe.js \
+            dist/profiles/e2-editor-v33 dist/profiles/ods-editor-v5; do
+  if [[ -e "$path" ]]; then
+    echo "Existing attempt found ($path). Preserve its files and report it; do not retry or clean." >&2
+    exit 1
+  fi
+done
+
+# Keep these builds independent of another session's make overrides.
+unset LOSRC LOBUILD EMSDK_ROOT EXTRA_DEFINES EXTRA_OBJECTS HOST_CXX MAKEFLAGS MFLAGS GNUMAKEFLAGS
+started=$(date +%s)
+make -j1 ALLOW_FROZEN_RELINK=1 build/e2/editor-v33/profile/sdk-manifest.json 2>&1 | tee "$writer_log"
+writer_done=$(date +%s)
+make -j1 build/ods/editor-v5/profile/sdk-manifest.json 2>&1 | tee "$calc_log"
+calc_done=$(date +%s)
+python3 - "$((writer_done - started))" "$((calc_done - writer_done))" <<'PY'
+import hashlib
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+sha = lambda path: hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+expected = {"e2-editor-v33": (2047 | (1 << 20) | (1 << 21), "docx"), "ods-editor-v5": (1046835 | (1 << 22) | (1 << 23), "xlsx")}
+report = {}
+for profile, seconds in (("build/e2/editor-v33/profile", sys.argv[1]), ("build/ods/editor-v5/profile", sys.argv[2])):
+    root = Path(profile)
+    manifest = json.loads((root / "sdk-manifest.json").read_text())
+    bits, ms = expected[manifest["profile"]]
+    report[manifest["profile"]] = {
+        "seconds": int(seconds),
+        "capabilityBits": manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"],
+        "capabilityBitsAsSpecified": manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"] == bits,
+        "msCapabilities": [c for c in manifest["capabilities"] if c.endswith(ms)],
+        **{name.replace(".", "_") + "Sha256": sha(root / name) for name in ("probe.wasm", "probe.js", "sdk-worker.js", "sdk-manifest.json")},
+    }
+print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
+ok = all(r["capabilityBitsAsSpecified"] and len(r["msCapabilities"]) == 2 for r in report.values())
+print("LINKS_AS_SPECIFIED" if ok else "LINKS_NOT_AS_SPECIFIED")
+sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)
+PY

+#ifndef WASM_SDK_PROBE_DOCUMENT_FORMAT_HPP
+#define WASM_SDK_PROBE_DOCUMENT_FORMAT_HPP
+
+// specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 5.1 E1: which names `oxsdk_document_open` accepts and which formats
+// `oxsdk_document_save` writes, per profile, as one closed table.  Only the OXSDK_MS_FORMATS builds
+// (Writer e2-editor-v33, Calc ods-editor-v5) include it; the older builds keep src/ods_viewer.hpp's
+// acceptedSuffix and the literal checks in src/sdk_api.cpp, byte for byte.
+//
+// Held by tests/document_format_test.cpp (native, no core); its red cases, one per table entry and
+// one per rule, are generated from this file by tests/document_format_mutations.py.
+//
+// finding 013's prescription: the name that passes validation is the name the input file gets in
+// MEMFS, and the format the open reply reports, so validation, path and capability say one thing.
+
+#include <cctype>
+#include <cstddef>
+#include <string>
+
+namespace probe::docformat {
+
+enum class Profile { Writer, Calc };
+
+struct Entry {
+  const char *suffix; // accepted at open, lower-case, with the dot
+  const char *format; // reported by the open reply and accepted by save
+  bool savable;       // the save boundary writes this format
+};
+
+// The table.  Calc keeps `.odt` at open because ods-editor-v4 accepts it (ods_viewer.hpp); it was
+// never savable there, and is not here.
+inline constexpr Entry kWriter[] = {
+    {".odt", "odt", true},
+    {".docx", "docx", true},
+};
+inline constexpr Entry kCalc[] = {
+    {".odt", "odt", false},
+    {".ods", "ods", true},
+    {".xlsx", "xlsx", true},
+};
+
+// The profile this build is: the Calc editor's rules define OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR, the Writer editor's do not.
+#ifdef OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR
+inline constexpr Profile kThisBuild = Profile::Calc;
+#else
+inline constexpr Profile kThisBuild = Profile::Writer;
+#endif
+
+template <std::size_t N>
+constexpr std::size_t countOf(const Entry (&)[N]) { return N; }
+
+inline const Entry *table(Profile profile, std::size_t &count) {
+  if (profile == Profile::Calc) {
+    count = countOf(kCalc);
+    return kCalc;
+  }
+  count = countOf(kWriter);
+  return kWriter;
+}
+
+inline std::string lower(std::string text) {
+  for (char &c : text)
+    c = static_cast<char>(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
+  return text;
+}
+
+// The table entry a name is accepted under, or nullptr.  Case does not matter (`REPORT.DOCX` is the
+// same file); a name must have at least one character before the suffix.
+inline const Entry *acceptedEntry(Profile profile, const std::string &name) {
+  std::size_t count = 0;
+  const Entry *entries = table(profile, count);
+  const std::string lowered = lower(name);
+  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+    const std::string suffix = entries[i].suffix;
+    if (lowered.size() > suffix.size() &&
+        lowered.compare(lowered.size() - suffix.size(), suffix.size(), suffix) == 0)
+      return &entries[i];
+  }
+  return nullptr;
+}
+
+// The suffix a name is accepted under ("" for a refusal), as ods_viewer.hpp's acceptedSuffix.
+inline std::string acceptedSuffix(Profile profile, const std::string &name) {
+  const Entry *entry = acceptedEntry(profile, name);
+  return entry ? entry->suffix : "";
+}
+
+// The format the open reply reports for an accepted name ("" for a refusal).
+inline std::string openFormat(Profile profile, const std::string &name) {
+  const Entry *entry = acceptedEntry(profile, name);
+  return entry ? entry->format : "";
+}
+
+// Whether the save boundary writes `format` (compared as written: the worker sends lower case).
+inline bool savable(Profile profile, const std::string &format) {
+  std::size_t count = 0;
+  const Entry *entries = table(profile, count);
+  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i)
+    if (entries[i].savable && format == entries[i].format)
+      return true;
+  return false;
+}
+
+} // namespace probe::docformat
+
+#endif

// specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 6.1 F3: the e2-editor-v33 and ods-editor-v5 workers in a vm sandbox.  Each is its frozen base plus the
// closed list in tests/v33-worker-delta.json / tests/ods-v5-worker-delta.json, derived the way tools/derive_ms_formats_worker.py
// derives it (the tool is run and must give the same bytes).  For each:
//   - a Microsoft-format name opens, and that format saves, only with the manifest's capability (UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION without it,
//     and the engine is never called);
//   - the ODF open and save keep their rows;
//   - the engine's `format` on `opened` reaches the open reply.
// Causal control: the frozen base runs the same exercise and must fail it (it gates nothing and drops `format`).
//   node tests/ms_formats_worker_test.cjs
const assert = require('node:assert/strict'), fs = require('node:fs'), vm = require('node:vm'), path = require('node:path');
const crypto = require('node:crypto'), os = require('node:os'), child = require('node:child_process');
const root = path.join(__dirname, '..');
const sha = (text) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(text).digest('hex');

const PROFILES = {
  writer: { base: 'dist/profiles/e2-editor-v32', baseSha: 'c394302c589baff3b40ff67a7579e07cf53fc7447dd3f04b26ff9c827280ff36',
            delta: 'tests/v33-worker-delta.json', ms: 'docx', odf: 'odt', profile: 'e2-editor-v33' },
  calc: { base: 'dist/profiles/ods-editor-v4', baseSha: '4a06fbe45c458b4c9aa60ab7bcd624894dace729a2b83f009f3e9008ccc8919c',
          delta: 'tests/ods-v5-worker-delta.json', ms: 'xlsx', odf: 'ods', profile: 'ods-editor-v5' },
};

function derive(name) {
  const p = PROFILES[name];
  const base = fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, p.base, 'sdk-worker.js'), 'utf8');
  assert.equal(sha(base), p.baseSha, `${name}: the frozen base worker moved`);
  let source = base;
  for (const h of JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, p.delta))).hunks) {
    assert.equal(source.split(h.old).length - 1, 1, `${name}: ${h.item}`);
    source = source.replace(h.old, () => h.new);
  }
  const out = path.join(os.tmpdir(), `ms-formats-worker-${name}-${process.pid}.js`);
  const tool = JSON.parse(child.execFileSync('python3', [path.join(root, 'tools/derive_ms_formats_worker.py'), '--profile', name, '--out', out], { encoding: 'utf8' }));
  fs.unlinkSync(out);
  assert.equal(tool.sha256, sha(source), `${name}: the tool derives another worker`);
  return { base, source };
}

function sandbox(src, manifestBase, capabilities, profile) {
  const manifest = { ...manifestBase, profile, capabilities };
  const messages = [], calls = [];
  let receive;
  const context = vm.createContext({ TextEncoder, console, self: { addEventListener: (_, fn) => receive = fn, postMessage: (m) => messages.push(m) } });
  vm.runInContext(src, context);
  context.fake = (name, ret, types, args) => {
    calls.push({ name, args });
    if (name === 'oxsdk_abi_version') return manifestBase.abiVersion;
    if (name === 'oxsdk_buffer_alloc') return 16;
    return 0;
  };
  context.manifest = manifest;
  context.instance = { ccall: context.fake, HEAPU8: new Uint8Array(1 << 16) };
  vm.runInContext('moduleInstance=instance;activeManifest=manifest;loadManifest=async()=>manifest;ensureModule=async()=>{};loadStartupResourcePacks=async()=>{};', context);
  let id = 1;
  const send = async (operation, payload, events = []) => {
    const requestId = id++;
    const before = calls.length;
    receive({ data: { protocolVersion: 1, kind: 'request', requestId, operation, payload } });
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    for (const event of events) context.handleCEvent(JSON.stringify({ ...event, requestId }));
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    return { reply: messages.filter((m) => m.kind === 'response' && m.requestId === requestId).at(-1),
             call: calls.slice(before).find((c) => c.name === `oxsdk_document_${operation}`) };
  };
  // The buffer is made in the sandbox's realm: the worker checks `instanceof ArrayBuffer`.
  return { send, buffer: () => vm.runInContext('new ArrayBuffer(8)', context) };
}

const opened = (format) => [
  { type: 'opened', documentHandle: 1, revision: 0, parts: 1, width: 100, height: 100, tileMode: 0, schemaVersion: 1, ...(format ? { format } : {}) },
  { type: 'view-ready', documentHandle: 1, revision: 0, schemaVersion: 1 },
];

async function exercise(name, src) {
  const p = PROFILES[name];
  const manifestBase = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, p.base, 'sdk-manifest.json')));
  const withMs = [...manifestBase.capabilities, `open-${p.ms}`, `save-${p.ms}`];
  // The Microsoft format, granted.
  const granted = sandbox(src, manifestBase, withMs, p.profile);
  const open = await granted.send('open', { buffer: granted.buffer(), name: `Report.${p.ms.toUpperCase()}` }, opened(p.ms));
  assert.ok(open.call, `${name}: a granted .${p.ms} open did not reach the engine`);
  assert.equal(open.reply.ok, true);
  assert.equal(open.reply.result.format, p.ms, `${name}: the engine's format did not reach the open reply`);
  const save = await granted.send('save', { documentHandle: 1, format: p.ms });
  assert.ok(save.call, `${name}: a granted ${p.ms} save did not reach the engine`);
  // Not granted: refused before the engine.
  const plain = sandbox(src, manifestBase, manifestBase.capabilities, p.profile);
  const refusedOpen = await plain.send('open', { buffer: plain.buffer(), name: `report.${p.ms}` });
  assert.equal(refusedOpen.call, undefined, `${name}: an ungranted .${p.ms} open reached the engine`);
  assert.equal(refusedOpen.reply.error.code, 'UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION');
  assert.equal(refusedOpen.reply.error.requiredCapability, `open-${p.ms}`);
  const refusedSave = await plain.send('save', { documentHandle: 1, format: p.ms });
  assert.equal(refusedSave.call, undefined, `${name}: an ungranted ${p.ms} save reached the engine`);
  assert.equal(refusedSave.reply.error.requiredCapability, `save-${p.ms}`);
  // ODF keeps its rows, and a reply without `format` keeps its shape.
  const odfOpen = await plain.send('open', { buffer: plain.buffer(), name: `report.${p.odf}` }, opened(null));
  assert.ok(odfOpen.call, `${name}: the .${p.odf} open was refused`);
  assert.equal(odfOpen.reply.ok, true);
  assert.equal('format' in odfOpen.reply.result, false, `${name}: a format appeared that the engine did not send`);
  const odfSave = await plain.send('save', { documentHandle: 1, format: p.odf });
  assert.ok(odfSave.call, `${name}: the ${p.odf} save was refused`);
}

(async () => {
  for (const name of Object.keys(PROFILES)) {
    const { base, source } = derive(name);
    await exercise(name, source);
    await assert.rejects(exercise(name, base), /reached the engine|did not reach the open reply/,
                         `causal control: the frozen ${name} base passed`);
  }
  console.log('ms-formats workers: deltas closed and derived by the tool; docx/xlsx open and save only with their capabilities; '
              + 'ODF rows unchanged; format reaches the open reply; both frozen bases fail the same exercise');
})().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exit(1); });
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 6.1 F2, before the links: em++ -fsyntax-only and -E on the e2-editor-v33 and ods-editor-v5 objects
(nothing produced, no make run; AGENTS.md 2026-10-28).

The compile commands are the Makefile's own, read from `make -n -B` of each profile's probe.js (a dry run prints them, runs
nothing), not rebuilt here.  For every command:

  syntax         `-c SRC -o OBJ` replaced by `-fsyntax-only SRC`, once as written and once without -DOXSDK_MS_FORMATS
                 (the predecessor's shape);
  control        sdk_api.cpp and probe_engine.cpp only: a scratch copy of src/ with an undeclared call inside the new
                 OXSDK_MS_FORMATS block must fail with the define and pass without it, or "green" could mean the block was
                 never compiled;
  exports        `-E -P`: every `_oxsdk_*` name in the profile's export list has a function body in the preprocessed sources
                 (a missing body fails the link and -fsyntax-only cannot see it, memory 2026-11-03).

taskset -c 0; refuses while a make runs.  Receipt: --out/report.json (argv, exit, seconds, first errors).
  check_ms_formats_syntax.py --out DIR
"""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import time

SDK = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROFILES = {
    "writer": {"target": "build/e2/editor-v33/probe.js", "exports": "build/archive/e2-editor-v32-54e50093-worker-c394302c/exports.txt"},
    "calc": {"target": "build/ods/editor-v5/probe.js", "exports": "build/ods/editor-v4/exports.txt"},
}
CONTROL = {  # a line inside each source's OXSDK_MS_FORMATS block, matched exactly once; the call goes right before it
    "sdk_api.cpp": "    if (probe::docformat::acceptedSuffix(probe::docformat::kThisBuild, name).empty())\n",
    "probe_engine.cpp": "  json << \",\\\"format\\\":\\\"\"\n",
}
CALL = "    thisFunctionDoesNotExist();\n"


def compile_commands(target):
    dry = subprocess.run(["make", "-n", "-B", target], capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=SDK)
    joined = re.sub(r"\\\n", " ", dry.stdout)
    commands = []
    for line in joined.splitlines():
        found = re.search(r"\s-c (src/\S+\.cpp) -o (\S+\.o)\s*$", line)
        if found and "em++" in line.split()[0]:
            commands.append({"source": found.group(1), "argv": line.split()})
    if not commands:
        raise SystemExit(f"no compile command found for {target}: {dry.stderr[-400:]}")
    return commands


def swap(argv, source, replacement):
    i = argv.index("-c")
    assert argv[i + 1] == source and argv[i + 2] == "-o"
    return argv[:i] + replacement + argv[i + 4:]


def run(argv, label, report):
    started = time.monotonic()
    done = subprocess.run(["taskset", "-c", "0", *argv], capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=SDK)
    errors = [line for line in done.stderr.splitlines() if re.search(r"\berror\b", line)]
    report["runs"].append({"label": label, "argv": argv, "exit": done.returncode,
                           "seconds": round(time.monotonic() - started, 1), "errors": errors[:8]})
    print(label, done.returncode, round(time.monotonic() - started, 1), errors[:1], flush=True)
    return done


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    out = args.out.resolve()
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    makes = subprocess.run(["pgrep", "-a", "make"], capture_output=True, text=True)
    report = {"uptime": subprocess.check_output(["uptime"], text=True).strip(), "makes": makes.stdout, "runs": [], "exports": {}}
    if makes.returncode == 0:
        (out / "report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=1) + "\n")
        raise SystemExit("a make is running; ask the owner first")
    scratch = out / "scratch"
    shutil.copytree(SDK / "src", scratch / "src")
    for name, source in CONTROL.items():
        path = scratch / "src" / name
        text = path.read_text()
        if text.count(source) != 1:
            raise SystemExit(f"control anchor for {name} occurs {text.count(source)} times")
        path.write_text(text.replace(source, CALL + source))
    verdict = {}
    for profile, spec in PROFILES.items():
        commands = compile_commands(spec["target"])
        report.setdefault("commands", {})[profile] = [c["source"] for c in commands]
        defined = set()
        for c in commands:
            argv, src = c["argv"], c["source"]
            name = Path(src).name
            with_define = "-DOXSDK_MS_FORMATS" in argv
            without = [a for a in argv if a != "-DOXSDK_MS_FORMATS"]
            run(swap(argv, src, ["-fsyntax-only", src]), f"{profile}/syntax/{name}", report)
            if with_define:
                run(swap(without, src, ["-fsyntax-only", src]), f"{profile}/syntax-without-define/{name}", report)
            if name in CONTROL and with_define:
                mutated = str(scratch / "src" / name)
                run(swap(argv, src, ["-fsyntax-only", mutated]), f"{profile}/control-with-define/{name}", report)
                run(swap(without, src, ["-fsyntax-only", mutated]), f"{profile}/control-without-define/{name}", report)
            pre = run(swap(argv, src, ["-E", "-P", src]), f"{profile}/preprocess/{name}", report)
            report["runs"][-1].pop("errors", None)
            defined |= set(re.findall(r"\b(oxsdk_[a-z0-9_]+)\s*\([^;{}]*\)\s*\{", pre.stdout))
        exported = sorted({n.lstrip("_") for n in (SDK / spec["exports"]).read_text().split() if n.startswith("_oxsdk_")})
        missing = [n for n in exported if n not in defined]
        report["exports"][profile] = {"list": spec["exports"], "exported": len(exported), "missing": missing}
        by = {r["label"]: r for r in report["runs"] if r["label"].startswith(profile + "/")}
        controls = [n for n in CONTROL if f"{profile}/control-with-define/{n}" in by]
        verdict[profile] = {
            "syntax-clean": all(r["exit"] == 0 for k, r in by.items() if "/syntax" in k),
            "controls-present": sorted(controls) == sorted(CONTROL),
            "control-caught-with-define": all(by[f"{profile}/control-with-define/{n}"]["exit"] != 0 and
                                              any("thisFunctionDoesNotExist" in e for e in by[f"{profile}/control-with-define/{n}"]["errors"])
                                              for n in controls),
            "control-silent-without-define": all(by[f"{profile}/control-without-define/{n}"]["exit"] == 0 for n in controls),
            "exports-have-bodies": bool(exported) and not missing,
        }
    shutil.rmtree(scratch)
    report["verdict"] = verdict
    (out / "report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=1) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps(verdict, indent=1))
    return 0 if all(all(v.values()) for v in verdict.values()) else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

     1	#ifndef WASM_SDK_PROBE_DOCUMENT_FORMAT_HPP
     2	#define WASM_SDK_PROBE_DOCUMENT_FORMAT_HPP
     3	
     4	// specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 5.1 E1: which names `oxsdk_document_open` accepts and which formats
     5	// `oxsdk_document_save` writes, per profile, as one closed table.  Only the OXSDK_MS_FORMATS builds
     6	// (Writer e2-editor-v33, Calc ods-editor-v5) include it; the older builds keep src/ods_viewer.hpp's
     7	// acceptedSuffix and the literal checks in src/sdk_api.cpp, byte for byte.
     8	//
     9	// Held by tests/document_format_test.cpp (native, no core); its red cases, one per table entry and
    10	// one per rule, are generated from this file by tests/document_format_mutations.py.
    11	//
    12	// finding 013's prescription: the name that passes validation is the name the input file gets in
    13	// MEMFS, and the format the open reply reports, so validation, path and capability say one thing.
    14	
    15	#include <cctype>
    16	#include <cstddef>
    17	#include <string>
    18	
    19	namespace probe::docformat {
    20	
    21	enum class Profile { Writer, Calc };
    22	
    23	struct Entry {
    24	  const char *suffix; // accepted at open, lower-case, with the dot
    25	  const char *format; // reported by the open reply and accepted by save
    26	  bool savable;       // the save boundary writes this format
    27	};
    28	
    29	// The table.  Calc keeps `.odt` at open because ods-editor-v4 accepts it (ods_viewer.hpp); it was
    30	// never savable there, and is not here.
    31	inline constexpr Entry kWriter[] = {
    32	    {".odt", "odt", true},
    33	    {".docx", "docx", true},
    34	};
    35	inline constexpr Entry kCalc[] = {
    36	    {".odt", "odt", false},
    37	    {".ods", "ods", true},
    38	    {".xlsx", "xlsx", true},
    39	};
    40	
    41	// The profile this build is: the Calc editor's rules define OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR, the Writer editor's do not.
    42	#ifdef OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR
    43	inline constexpr Profile kThisBuild = Profile::Calc;
    44	#else
    45	inline constexpr Profile kThisBuild = Profile::Writer;
    46	#endif
    47	
    48	template <std::size_t N>
    49	constexpr std::size_t countOf(const Entry (&)[N]) { return N; }
    50	
    51	inline const Entry *table(Profile profile, std::size_t &count) {
    52	  if (profile == Profile::Calc) {
    53	    count = countOf(kCalc);
    54	    return kCalc;
    55	  }
    56	  count = countOf(kWriter);
    57	  return kWriter;
    58	}
    59	
    60	inline std::string lower(std::string text) {
    61	  for (char &c : text)
    62	    c = static_cast<char>(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
    63	  return text;
    64	}
    65	
    66	// The table entry a name is accepted under, or nullptr.  Case does not matter (`REPORT.DOCX` is the
    67	// same file); a name must have at least one character before the suffix.
    68	inline const Entry *acceptedEntry(Profile profile, const std::string &name) {
    69	  std::size_t count = 0;
    70	  const Entry *entries = table(profile, count);
    71	  const std::string lowered = lower(name);
    72	  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
    73	    const std::string suffix = entries[i].suffix;
    74	    if (lowered.size() > suffix.size() &&
    75	        lowered.compare(lowered.size() - suffix.size(), suffix.size(), suffix) == 0)
    76	      return &entries[i];
    77	  }
    78	  return nullptr;
    79	}
    80	
    81	// The suffix a name is accepted under ("" for a refusal), as ods_viewer.hpp's acceptedSuffix.
    82	inline std::string acceptedSuffix(Profile profile, const std::string &name) {
    83	  const Entry *entry = acceptedEntry(profile, name);
    84	  return entry ? entry->suffix : "";
    85	}
    86	
    87	// The format the open reply reports for an accepted name ("" for a refusal).
    88	inline std::string openFormat(Profile profile, const std::string &name) {
    89	  const Entry *entry = acceptedEntry(profile, name);
    90	  return entry ? entry->format : "";
    91	}
    92	
    93	// Whether the save boundary writes `format` (compared as written: the worker sends lower case).
    94	inline bool savable(Profile profile, const std::string &format) {
    95	  std::size_t count = 0;
    96	  const Entry *entries = table(profile, count);
    97	  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i)
    98	    if (entries[i].savable && format == entries[i].format)
    99	      return true;
   100	  return false;
   101	}
   102	
   103	} // namespace probe::docformat
   104	
   105	#endif

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Assemble the Microsoft-formats successors (specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 5.1) without overwriting a frozen profile.  Derived from
     3	build_calc_undo_profile.py.
     4	
     5	Closed list of differences from the frozen base manifest:
     6	
     7	  writer  base dist/profiles/e2-editor-v32  -> e2-editor-v33: capabilities + open-docx, save-docx; bits 20, 21
     8	  calc    base dist/profiles/ods-editor-v4  -> ods-editor-v5: capabilities + open-xlsx, save-xlsx; bits 22, 23
     9	
    10	plus `profile`, `sdkVersion` and a `documentFormatContract` naming the table (src/document_format.hpp).  The export list must equal
    11	the base's (no new export), and the base must not already claim either capability.  --development assembles under build/ only;
    12	without it the output must not already hold a manifest (a frozen profile is never overwritten).
    13	  build_ms_formats_profile.py --profile writer|calc --loader F --wasm F --worker F --exports F --base-exports F --output DIR [--development]
    14	"""
    15	import argparse
    16	import hashlib
    17	import json
    18	from pathlib import Path
    19	import shutil
    20	
    21	HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    22	PROFILES = {
    23	    "writer": {"base": HERE / "dist/profiles/e2-editor-v32", "profile": "e2-editor-v33", "ms": "docx",
    24	               "bits": (1 << 20) | (1 << 21),  # src/sdk_api.h OXSDK_CAPABILITY_OPEN_DOCX, SAVE_DOCX
    25	               "open": [".odt", ".docx"], "save": ["odt", "docx"]},
    26	    "calc": {"base": HERE / "dist/profiles/ods-editor-v4", "profile": "ods-editor-v5", "ms": "xlsx",
    27	             "bits": (1 << 22) | (1 << 23),  # OXSDK_CAPABILITY_OPEN_XLSX, SAVE_XLSX
    28	             "open": [".odt", ".ods", ".xlsx"], "save": ["ods", "xlsx"]},
    29	}
    30	
    31	
    32	def main():
    33	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    34	    parser.add_argument("--profile", choices=sorted(PROFILES), required=True)
    35	    for name in ("loader", "wasm", "worker", "exports", "base-exports", "output"):
    36	        parser.add_argument("--" + name, type=Path, required=True)
    37	    parser.add_argument("--development", action="store_true")
    38	    args = parser.parse_args()
    39	    spec = PROFILES[args.profile]
    40	    base = spec["base"]
    41	    output = args.output.resolve()
    42	    if args.development and not output.is_relative_to((HERE / "build").resolve()):
    43	        raise SystemExit("development assembly must stay under build; frozen profiles are immutable")
    44	    if (output / "sdk-manifest.json").exists() and not args.development:
    45	        raise SystemExit("profile already exists; a frozen profile is not overwritten")
    46	    if output == base.resolve():
    47	        raise SystemExit("the predecessor profile must never be overwritten")
    48	    exports, base_exports = set(args.exports.read_text().split()), set(args.base_exports.read_text().split())
    49	    if exports != base_exports:
    50	        raise SystemExit(f"the export list moved: added {sorted(exports - base_exports)}, removed {sorted(base_exports - exports)}")
    51	    manifest = json.loads((base / "sdk-manifest.json").read_text())
    52	    ms = spec["ms"]
    53	    if {f"open-{ms}", f"save-{ms}"} & set(manifest["capabilities"]) or manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"] & spec["bits"]:
    54	        raise SystemExit(f"the base already claims {ms}")
    55	    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    56	    for name, source in (("probe.js", args.loader), ("probe.wasm", args.wasm), ("sdk-worker.js", args.worker)):
    57	        shutil.copyfile(source, output / name)
    58	    for key in ("soffice.data", "soffice.data.js.metadata"):
    59	        relative = manifest["artifactFiles"][key]
    60	        if relative.startswith("./"):  # Calc carries its image beside the engine; Writer's lives in ../resources
    61	            shutil.copyfile(base / relative[2:], output / relative[2:])
    62	    manifest["profile"] = spec["profile"]
    63	    manifest["sdkVersion"] = "0.6.0-r6-worker+" + spec["profile"]
    64	    manifest["capabilities"] += [f"open-{ms}", f"save-{ms}"]
    65	    manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"] |= spec["bits"]
    66	    manifest["documentFormatContract"] = {
    67	        "version": 1, "table": "src/document_format.hpp", "open": spec["open"], "save": spec["save"],
    68	        "openReply": "format: the accepted suffix's format", "refused": "INVALID_ARGUMENT before the queue",
    69	    }
    70	    (output / "sdk-manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + "\n")
    71	    print(json.dumps({"profile": spec["profile"], "development": args.development,
    72	                      "capabilityBits": manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"],
    73	                      "sha256": {name: hashlib.sha256((output / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    74	                                 for name in ("probe.js", "probe.wasm", "sdk-worker.js", "sdk-manifest.json")}}, indent=2))
    75	
    76

   110	         OXSDK_CAPABILITY_REPLACE_SELECTION | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_UNDO |
   111	         OXSDK_CAPABILITY_COMMENTS | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_TRACKED_CHANGES
   112	#ifdef OXSDK_MS_FORMATS
   113	         | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_OPEN_DOCX | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_SAVE_DOCX
   114	#endif
   115	         ;
   116	#endif
   117	}
   118	
   119	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_engine_start(
   120	    std::uint32_t requestedAbiVersion, oxsdk_request_id requestId) {
   121	  if (!validRequest(requestId))
   122	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
   123	  if (!compatibleVersion(requestedAbiVersion))
   124	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INCOMPATIBLE_ABI;
   125	  return safeStatus([&] { return probe::start(requestId); });
   126	}
   127	
   128	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_open(
   129	    oxsdk_request_id requestId, const std::uint8_t *bytes,
   130	    std::uint32_t byteLength, const char *nameUtf8, std::uint32_t nameLength) {
   131	  if (!validRequest(requestId) || !bytes || byteLength == 0 || !nameUtf8 ||
   132	      nameLength == 0 || nameLength > 255) {
   133	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  return safeStatus([&] {
   137	    std::string name(nameUtf8, nameLength);
   138	#ifdef OXSDK_MS_FORMATS
   139	    // SPEC-P-ms-formats 5.1 E1 (finding 013's narrow surface): the table, per build.
   140	    if (probe::docformat::acceptedSuffix(probe::docformat::kThisBuild, name).empty())
   141	      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
   142	#elif defined(OXSDK_ODS_VIEWER)
   143	    // W7a (finding 013's allowlist): `.ods` beside `.odt`, either case.
   144	    // Content detection still reads the zip's own `mimetype`.
   145	    if (probe::odsviewer::acceptedSuffix(name).empty())
   146	      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
   147	#else
   148	    if (name.size() < 4 || name.substr(name.size() - 4) != ".odt")
   149	      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
   150	#endif
   151	    return probe::openBytes(
   152	        requestId, std::vector<std::uint8_t>(bytes, bytes + byteLength),
   153	        std::move(name));
   154	  });
   155	}
   156	
   157	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_paint(
   158	    oxsdk_request_id requestId, oxsdk_document_handle documentHandle,
   159	    std::int32_t xTwips, std::int32_t yTwips, std::int32_t widthTwips,
   160	    std::int32_t heightTwips, std::int32_t canvasWidthPx,
   161	    std::int32_t canvasHeightPx) {
   162	  if (!validRequest(requestId) || documentHandle == 0 || xTwips < 0 ||
   163	      yTwips < 0 || widthTwips <= 0 || heightTwips <= 0 || canvasWidthPx <= 0 ||
   164	      canvasHeightPx <= 0) {
   165	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
   166	  }
   167	  return safeStatus([&] {
   168	    return probe::paintTile(requestId, documentHandle, xTwips, yTwips,
   169	                            widthTwips, heightTwips, canvasWidthPx,
   170	                            canvasHeightPx);
   171	  });
   172	}
   173	
   174	#ifndef OXSDK_READER_PROFILE
   175	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_click(
   176	    oxsdk_request_id requestId, oxsdk_document_handle documentHandle,
   177	    std::int32_t xTwips, std::int32_t yTwips) {
   178	  if (!validRequest(requestId) || documentHandle == 0 || xTwips < 0 ||
   179	      yTwips < 0)
   180	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
   181	  return safeStatus(
   182	      [&] { return probe::click(requestId, documentHandle, xTwips, yTwips); });
   183	}
   184	
   185	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_insert_text(
   186	    oxsdk_request_id requestId, oxsdk_document_handle documentHandle,
   187	    const char *utf8, std::uint32_t utf8Length) {
   188	  if (!validRequest(requestId) || documentHandle == 0 || !utf8 ||
   189	      utf8Length == 0)
   190	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
   191	  return safeStatus([&] {
   192	    return probe::insertText(requestId, documentHandle,
   193	                             std::string(utf8, utf8Length));
   194	  });
   195	}
   196	#endif
   197	
   198	// The viewer has no save (SPEC-M4 3.2, review M2): not in its export list and
   199	// not compiled, because EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE exports a function whatever the
   200	// list says -- the reader's click is kept out the same way.
   201	#if !defined(OXSDK_ODS_VIEWER) || defined(OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR)
   202	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_save(
   203	    oxsdk_request_id requestId, oxsdk_document_handle documentHandle,
   204	    const char *formatUtf8, std::uint32_t formatLength) {
   205	  if (!validRequest(requestId) || documentHandle == 0 || !formatUtf8 ||
   206	      formatLength == 0)
   207	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
   208	  return safeStatus([&] {
   209	    const std::string format(formatUtf8, formatLength);
   210	#ifdef OXSDK_MS_FORMATS
   211	    if (!probe::docformat::savable(probe::docformat::kThisBuild, format))
   212	#elif defined(OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR)
   213	    if (format != "ods")
   214	#else
   215	    if (format != "odt")
   216	#endif
   217	      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
   218	    return probe::save(requestId, documentHandle, format);
   219	  });
   220	}
   221	#endif
   222	
   223	#ifdef OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR
   224	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_cell(
   225	    oxsdk_request_id requestId, oxsdk_document_handle documentHandle,
   226	    std::uint32_t expectedRevision, std::int32_t part, std::int32_t column,
   227	    std::int32_t row, std::int32_t operation, const char *text,
   228	    std::uint32_t textLength, std::uint32_t enabled) {
   229	  constexpr int maxOperation =
   230	#ifdef OXSDK_ODS_STYLE
   231	      12;
   232	#elif defined(OXSDK_ODS_RANGE)
   233	      9;
   234	#else
   235	      5;
   236	#endif
   237	  if (!validRequest(requestId) || documentHandle == 0 || part < 0
   238	      || column < 0 || row < 0 || operation < 0 || operation > maxOperation
   239	      || enabled > 1 || (textLength > 0 && !text) || textLength > 131068)
   240	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
   241	  return safeStatus([&] {
   242	    return probe::cell(requestId, documentHandle, expectedRevision, part,
   243	        column, row, operation, std::string(text ? text : "", textLength), enabled != 0);
   244	  });
   245	}

    "emptyStack": "NOTHING_TO_UNDO / NOTHING_TO_REDO before dispatch",
    "proof": "the undo and redo lists move by exactly one, else MUTATION_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN",
    "reply": [
      "revision",
      "undoCount",
      "redoCount",
      "cursor {part, column, row, moved} or null"
    ],
    "singleCellBoldItalic": "one undo action (one setPropertyValues)"
  }

     2	// LOK: dispatch reads are owned values, flush-dependent fields use live caches.
     3	void emitOrderedMissingProof(const Command &command, const char *operation,
     4	                             const char *callback, const char *code = "MUTATION_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN") {

    17	void composeOrderedEditorPending(EditorPendingOperation pending) {
    18	  int matched = -1;
    19	  for (int type : {LOK_CALLBACK_INVALIDATE_VISIBLE_CURSOR, LOK_CALLBACK_TEXT_SELECTION}) {
    20	    const auto found = gOrderedProof.callbacks.find(type);
    21	    if (found != gOrderedProof.callbacks.end() && found->second > pending.beforeSequence &&
    22	        (pending.requiredCallback == type || pending.requiredCallback == EditorCaretOrSelectionCallback)) {
    23	      matched = type;
    24	      break;
    25	    }
    26	  }
    27	  const char *completion = matched == LOK_CALLBACK_INVALIDATE_VISIBLE_CURSOR
    28	      ? "documented-callback-visible-cursor"
    29	      : matched == LOK_CALLBACK_TEXT_SELECTION ? "documented-callback-text-selection"
    30	      : pending.caret ? "verified-caret-readback"
    31	      : pending.selection ? "verified-selection-readback" : "verified-move-readback";
    32	  std::ostringstream json;
    33	  json << "{\"schemaVersion\":" << ProtocolSchemaVersion << ",\"type\":\""
    34	       << (pending.caret ? "editor-caret-placed" : pending.selection
    35	           ? "editor-selection-completed" : "editor-action-completed")
    36	       << "\",\"requestId\":" << pending.requestId
    37	       << ",\"documentHandle\":" << pending.documentHandle
    38	       << ",\"revision\":" << gState.revision;
    39	  if (pending.selection)
    40	    json << ",\"method\":\"" << jsonEscape(pending.name.c_str()) << "\"";
    41	  else if (!pending.caret)
    42	    json << ",\"beforeRevision\":" << pending.beforeRevision
    43	         << ",\"action\":\"" << jsonEscape(pending.name.c_str())
    44	         << "\",\"option\":" << (pending.option ? "true" : "false") << ",\"changed\":false";
    45	  json << ",\"completion\":\"" << completion
    46	       << "\",\"callbackSequenceBefore\":" << pending.beforeSequence
    47	       << ",\"callbackSequenceAfter\":" << gEditorState.sourceSequence << ",\"state\":{";
    48	  appendEditorState(json);
    49	  json << "}}";
    50	  emitJson(json.str());
    51	  gEditorPending = EditorPendingOperation{};
    52	}
    53	
    54	#ifdef OXSDK_E2_V25_FORMAT_PROOF
    55	// Finding 172, SPEC-M3-ordered-commands section 23.  What makes an inline-format
    56	// request true, given that core answers `success:false` when it honours the
    57	// parameterised form (finding 059):
    58	//   uno-success   the result itself says so;
    59	//   on-time-item  this flush carries the state item and it equals the request;
    60	//   state-noop    this flush carries NO item, the item is on the measured
    61	//                 on-time list (core broadcasts a change in the command's own
    62	//                 flush, 5/5), and the known state already equals the request:
    63	//                 nothing changed because nothing had to.  Without this case a
    64	//                 request for the current state -- clear-format's first press
    65	//                 on plain text -- answers LOK_COMMAND_FAILED.
    66	// Named limit L-noop-trusts-last-state-item: a stale cache plus a refused
    67	// dispatch passes here, exactly as it did on v22.
    68	bool orderedInlineFormatStateEquals(const std::string &unoCommand, bool requested) {
    69	  if (unoCommand == ".uno:Bold")
    70	    return gEditorState.boldKnown && gEditorState.bold == requested;
    71	  if (unoCommand == ".uno:Italic")
    72	    return gEditorState.italicKnown && gEditorState.italic == requested;
    73	  if (unoCommand == ".uno:Underline")
    74	    return gEditorState.underlineKnown && gEditorState.underline == requested;
    75	  if (unoCommand == ".uno:Strikeout")
    76	    return gEditorState.strikeoutKnown && gEditorState.strikeout == requested;
    77	  return false;
    78	}
    79	
    80	const char *orderedInlineFormatWitness(const std::string &unoCommand, bool requested,
    81	                                       const std::string &payload) {
    82	  if (commandResultSucceeded(payload.c_str()))
    83	    return "uno-success";
    84	  for (const auto &read : probe::replystate::kReads) {
    85	    if (unoCommand != read.unoCommand)
    86	      continue;
    87	    if (gOrderedProof.stateItems.count(read.stateKey))
    88	      return orderedInlineFormatStateEquals(unoCommand, requested) ? "on-time-item" : nullptr;
    89	    return orderedInlineFormatStateEquals(unoCommand, requested) ? "state-noop" : nullptr;
    90	  }
    91	  return nullptr;
    92	}
    93	#endif
    94	
    95	void composeOrderedUno(const Command &command, const std::string &operation,
    96	                       const std::string &unoCommand, const std::string &action,
    97	                       std::uint32_t beforeRevision, std::uint64_t beforeSequence,
    98	                       bool option) {
    99	  std::string payload;
   100	  if (!orderedUnoResult(unoCommand, payload)) {
   101	    emitOrderedMissingProof(command, operation.c_str(), "UNO_COMMAND_RESULT");
   102	    return;
   103	  }
   104	  if (operation == "comment-added") {
   105	    if (!gOrderedProof.commentAdded) {
   106	      emitOrderedMissingProof(command, operation.c_str(), "COMMENT", "LOK_ERROR");
   107	      return;
   108	    }
   109	    payload = gOrderedProof.commentPayload;
   110	  }
   111	  if (operation == "editor-action-completed") {
   112	#ifdef OXSDK_E2_V25_FORMAT_PROOF
   113	    const char *witness = orderedInlineFormatWitness(unoCommand, option, payload);
   114	    if (!witness) {
   115	      emitSdkError(command.requestId, command.documentHandle, "editor-action",
   116	                   "LOK_COMMAND_FAILED", "LibreOfficeKit rejected the fixed editor command");
   117	      return;
   118	    }
   119	#else
   120	    // 059 uses only an on-time item observed in this command's own flush.
   121	    if (!commandResultSucceeded(payload.c_str()) &&
   122	        !inlineFormatArgumentResolved(unoCommand, option)) {
   123	      emitSdkError(command.requestId, command.documentHandle, "editor-action",
   124	                   "LOK_COMMAND_FAILED", "LibreOfficeKit rejected the fixed editor command");
   125	      return;
   126	    }
   127	#endif
   128	    advanceRevision();
   129	    std::ostringstream json;
   130	    json << "{\"schemaVersion\":" << ProtocolSchemaVersion
   131	         << ",\"type\":\"editor-action-completed\",\"requestId\":" << command.requestId
   132	         << ",\"documentHandle\":" << command.documentHandle
   133	         << ",\"beforeRevision\":" << beforeRevision << ",\"revision\":" << gState.revision
   134	         << ",\"action\":\"" << jsonEscape(action.c_str())
   135	         << "\",\"option\":" << (option ? "true" : "false")
   136	         << ",\"changed\":true,\"completion\":\"uno-command-result\""
   137	#ifdef OXSDK_E2_V25_FORMAT_PROOF
   138	         << ",\"witness\":\"" << witness << "\""
   139	#endif
   140	         << ",\"callbackSequenceBefore\":" << beforeSequence
   141	         << ",\"callbackSequenceAfter\":" << gEditorState.sourceSequence << ",\"state\":{";
   142	    appendEditorState(json);
   143	    json << "}}";
   144	    emitJson(json.str());
   145	    return;
   146	  }
   147	  // Undo/redo/track changes retain the existing void-command result policy.
   148	  advanceRevision();
   149	  std::ostringstream json;
   150	  json << "{\"schemaVersion\":" << ProtocolSchemaVersion << ",\"type\":\""
   151	       << jsonEscape(operation.c_str()) << "\",\"requestId\":" << command.requestId
   152	       << ",\"documentHandle\":" << command.documentHandle
   153	       << ",\"revision\":" << gState.revision << ",\"resultPayload\":\""
   154	       << jsonEscape(payload.c_str()) << "\"}";
   155	  emitJson(json.str());
   156	}

 exited 2 in 0ms:
      || pack.bytes !== buffer.byteLength) {
    throw new Error(`resource pack ${pack.id} size or metadata mismatch`);
  }
  const expectedHash = String(pack.sha256 || "").toLowerCase();
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(expectedHash)
      || expectedHash !== String(metadata.sha256 || "").toLowerCase()
      || await sha256Hex(buffer) !== expectedHash) {
    throw new Error(`resource pack ${pack.id} SHA-256 mismatch`);
  }

  const paths = new Set();
  let previousEnd = 0;
  for (const file of metadata.files) {
    if (!safeResourcePath(file.filename) || paths.has(file.filename)
        || !Number.isInteger(file.start) || !Number.isInteger(file.end)
        || file.start !== previousEnd || file.end <= file.start
        || file.end > bytes.byteLength) {
      throw new Error(`resource pack ${pack.id} contains an invalid file range or path`);

    throw new Error(`resource pack ${pack.id} does not cover its complete data blob`);
