    moduleInstance.FS.mkdirTree(directory);
    moduleInstance.FS.writeFile(file.filename, bytes.subarray(file.start, file.end));
  }
  loadedResourcePacks.add(pack.id);
  postEvent("resource-pack-loaded", {
    resourcePack: pack.id,
    files: metadata.files.length,
    bytes: buffer.byteLength,
    sha256: expectedHash,
  });
}

async function loadStartupResourcePacks(manifest) {
  for (const pack of manifest.resourcePacks || []) {
    if (pack.loadAtStartup === true)
      await loadResourcePack(pack);
  }
}

function ensureModule(manifest) {
  if (modulePromise)
    return modulePromise;
  modulePromise = (async () => {
    self.__probe_on_event = handleCEvent;
    const artifactFiles = manifest.artifactFiles || {};
    const loaderUrl = new URL(artifactFiles["probe.js"] || "./probe.js", self.location.href).href;
    importScripts(loaderUrl);
    const factory = self.createProbeModule;
    if (typeof factory !== "function")
      throw new Error("probe.js did not expose createProbeModule in the worker");
    moduleInstance = await factory({
      // importScripts() leaves self.location at sdk-worker.js. Without this
      // override Emscripten's pthread pool would spawn sdk-worker.js again
      // instead of loading the generated pthread entrypoint in probe.js.
      mainScriptUrlOrBlob: loaderUrl,
      print: (text) => {
        if (debugEnabled)
          postEvent("diagnostic", { level: "stdout", message: String(text) });
      },
      printErr: (text) => {
        if (debugEnabled)
          postEvent("diagnostic", { level: "stderr", message: String(text) });
      },
      locateFile: (path) => new URL(artifactFiles[path] || path, self.location.href).href,
    });
    return moduleInstance;
  })();
  return modulePromise;
}

function handleCEvent(rawEvent) {
  let event;
  try {
    event = JSON.parse(rawEvent);
  } catch (error) {
    postEvent("worker-protocol-error", { message: String(error), rawEvent });
    return;
  }

  const requestId = Number(event.requestId || 0);
  switch (event.type) {
    case "ready": {
      if (!requestId)
        return;
      Promise.all([loadManifest(), Promise.resolve({
        abiVersion: event.abiVersion,
        capabilityBits: event.capabilities,
      })]).then(([manifest, runtime]) => {
        if (Number.isInteger(manifest.expectedCapabilityBits)
            && manifest.expectedCapabilityBits !== runtime.capabilityBits) {
          throw new Error(
            `manifest capability bits ${manifest.expectedCapabilityBits} do not match runtime ${runtime.capabilityBits}`,
          );
        }
        complete(requestId, { ...manifest, ...runtime });
      }).catch((error) => {
        fail(requestId, "MANIFEST_ERROR", String(error?.stack || error));
      });
      break;
    }
    case "opened": {
      if (!requestId)
        return;
          // Markdown paste says `dispatched: false`, so a host can tell "the
          // document was not touched" without knowing the code.  A boolean or
          // nothing: an engine that does not send it has not said.
          dispatched: typeof event.dispatched === "boolean" ? event.dispatched : undefined,
        });
      } else {
        postEvent("engine-error", {
          error: { code: event.code || "ENGINE_ERROR", message: event.message || event.msg },
        });
      }
      break;
    case "lok":
      if (event.id === 0 || event.id === 1) {
        postEvent("document-invalidated", {
          documentHandle: event.documentHandle,
          revision: event.revision,
        });
      } else if (debugEnabled && [12, 15, 16, 30, 31, 32].includes(event.id)) {
        postEvent("diagnostic", { level: "lok-review", detail: event });
      }
      break;
    case "stage":
      if (debugEnabled)
        postEvent("diagnostic", { level: "stage", detail: event });
      break;
    default:
      if (debugEnabled)
        postEvent("diagnostic", { level: "event", detail: event });
  }
}

async function handleInit(request) {
  debugEnabled = request.payload?.debug === true;
  pending.set(request.requestId, { operation: request.operation });
  try {
    const manifest = await loadManifest();
    activeManifest = manifest;
    await ensureModule(manifest);
    await loadStartupResourcePacks(manifest);
    const actualAbi = Number(ccall("oxsdk_abi_version", "number"));
    const requestedAbi = Number(request.payload.requestedAbiVersion);
    const compatibleAbi = (actualAbi >>> 16) === (requestedAbi >>> 16)
                          && (requestedAbi & 0xffff) <= (actualAbi & 0xffff);
    if (!compatibleAbi) {
      fail(request.requestId, "INCOMPATIBLE_ABI",
           `worker ABI ${actualAbi} is not compatible with requested ${requestedAbi}`);
      return;
    }
    // SPEC E2-B 5.4: the editor ABI is versioned separately, and the manifest's
    // abiVersion is a CLAIM while the binary's is a fact.  Findings 027/036 are
    // about those two coming apart -- a profile is assembled, the hash is not a
    // function of the source, and a stale wasm beside a fresh manifest runs
    // today with nobody the wiser.  Exact match, because an allowlist's
    // version is an identity, not a range.
    const contract = activeManifest?.editorContract;
    if (contract && typeof contract.abiVersion === "number") {
      const actualEditorAbi = Number(ccall("oxsdk_editor_abi_version", "number"));
      if (actualEditorAbi !== contract.abiVersion) {
        fail(request.requestId, "INCOMPATIBLE_ABI",
             `editor ABI ${actualEditorAbi} in this binary does not match `
             + `${contract.abiVersion} declared by profile `
             + `${activeManifest?.profile || "unknown"}`);
        return;
      }
    }

    // SPEC-M3-link-batch-v24 4.2 (fable ruling 2): what an ABI bump would
    // have caught at init.  A declaration is a claim about the binary; a
    // stale wasm beside a fresh manifest must be refused here, not surface as
    // WORKER_EXCEPTION at the first typed character.
    for (const spec of DOCUMENT_EXPORT_OPERATIONS.values()) {
      if (contract?.[spec.field] === spec.declaration
          && typeof moduleInstance?.["_" + spec.symbol] !== "function") {
        fail(request.requestId, "DECLARED_EXPORT_MISSING",
             `profile ${activeManifest?.profile || "unknown"} declares `
             + `${spec.field}: "${spec.declaration}" but this binary has no `
             + `${spec.symbol}`);
        return;
      }
    }

    // SPEC E2-B 5.7: push the manifest's gesture restrictions into the engine.
    //
    // The engine reads no manifest, and the mask cannot ride on a dispatch
    // because both option flags must be 0 for the paragraph actions.  Without
    // this call the `gestures` field is decorative: it would describe a
    // narrowing that nothing enforces, which is the defect 5.7 exists to
    // remove.  Narrowing only -- the engine intersects.
    //
    // SPEC-M3-link-batch-v21 9.7 / 11.1 D12: the call's STATUS is read, and
    // anything but OK fails the init.  It used to be ignored, and the engine
    // refuses an id above its `kMaxExternalAction` with INVALID_ARGUMENT: a
    // binary whose bound did not move with the action list would have stored
    // no mask for the new ids, so the engine's gate would refuse them every
    // time while the manifest said they were granted -- the silent, total
    // failure CLAUDE.md's "the bound follows the list" names.  A manifest the
    // binary cannot narrow is a manifest that does not describe this binary.
    const actions = contract?.actions;
    const bits = contract?.gestureBits;
    if (actions && bits && !Array.isArray(actions)) {
      for (const [name, spec] of Object.entries(actions)) {
        if (!Number.isInteger(spec?.id) || !Array.isArray(spec?.gestures))
          continue;
        let mask = 0;
        for (const gesture of spec.gestures)
          mask |= Number(bits[gesture]) || 0;
        const maskStatus = callStatus("oxsdk_editor_set_action_gestures",
                                      ["number", "number"], [spec.id, mask]);
        if (maskStatus !== 0) {
          fail(request.requestId, "INCOMPATIBLE_ABI",
               `the binary refused the gesture mask for ${name} (id ${spec.id}, `
               + `mask ${mask}): ${STATUS_NAMES.get(maskStatus) || maskStatus}. `
               + `Profile ${activeManifest?.profile || "unknown"} declares an `
               + "action this binary cannot narrow",
               { action: name, actionId: spec.id, mask, status: maskStatus });
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    const status = callStatus(
      "oxsdk_engine_start",
      ["number", "number"],
      [requestedAbi, request.requestId],
    );
    if (status !== 0) {
      fail(request.requestId, STATUS_NAMES.get(status) || "UNKNOWN_STATUS",
           "engine start was rejected", { status });
    }
  } catch (error) {
    fail(request.requestId, "WORKER_INIT_FAILED", String(error?.stack || error));
  }
}

function handleRequest(request) {
  if (request.operation === "init") {
    void handleInit(request);
    return;
  }
  if (!moduleInstance) {
    postResponse(request.requestId, false, {
      code: "NOT_INITIALIZED",
      message: "worker must complete init before document operations",
    });
    return;
  }

  const payload = request.payload || {};
  const requiredCapability = OPERATION_CAPABILITIES.get(request.operation);
  if (requiredCapability
      && !activeManifest?.capabilities?.includes(requiredCapability)) {
    postResponse(request.requestId, false, {
      code: "UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION",
      message: `${request.operation} is unavailable in profile ${activeManifest?.profile || "unknown"}`,
      operation: request.operation,
      requiredCapability,
      profile: activeManifest?.profile,
    });
    return;
  }
  if (request.operation.startsWith("editorDiscovery")
      && !editorDiscoveryEnabled()) {
    postResponse(request.requestId, false, {
      code: "UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION",
      message: "editor discovery operations require the isolated E1 diagnostic profile",
      operation: request.operation,
      accept(request, () => callStatus(
        "oxsdk_editor_discovery_get_state",
        ["number", "number"],
        [request.requestId, payload.documentHandle],
      ));
      break;
    case "finding016DrainScheduler":
      accept(request, () => callStatus(
        "oxsdk_editor_discovery_drain_scheduler",
        ["number", "number"],
        [request.requestId, payload.documentHandle],
      ));
      break;
    case "save":
      accept(request, () => withUtf8(String(payload.format || ""), (pointer, length) =>
        callStatus(
          "oxsdk_document_save",
          ["number", "number", "number", "number"],
          [request.requestId, payload.documentHandle, pointer, length],
        )));
      break;
    case "close":
      accept(request, () => callStatus(
        "oxsdk_document_close",
        ["number", "number"],
        [request.requestId, payload.documentHandle],
      ));
      break;
    default:
      postResponse(request.requestId, false, {
        code: "UNKNOWN_OPERATION",
        message: `unknown worker operation: ${request.operation}`,
      });
  }
}

function handleCancel(message) {
  if (!moduleInstance) {
    postEvent("cancel-result", {
      requestId: message.requestId,
      status: "NOT_STARTED",
    });
    return;
  }
  const status = callStatus("oxsdk_request_cancel", ["number"], [message.requestId]);
  postEvent("cancel-result", {
    requestId: message.requestId,
    status: STATUS_NAMES.get(status) || "UNKNOWN_STATUS",
  });
}

    73	async function exercise(name, src) {
    74	  const p = PROFILES[name];
    75	  const manifestBase = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, p.base, 'sdk-manifest.json')));
    76	  const withMs = [...manifestBase.capabilities, `open-${p.ms}`, `save-${p.ms}`];
    77	  // The Microsoft format, granted.
    78	  const granted = sandbox(src, manifestBase, withMs, p.profile);
    79	  const open = await granted.send('open', { buffer: granted.buffer(), name: `Report.${p.ms.toUpperCase()}` }, opened(p.ms));
    80	  assert.ok(open.call, `${name}: a granted .${p.ms} open did not reach the engine`);
    81	  assert.equal(open.reply.ok, true);
    82	  assert.equal(open.reply.result.format, p.ms, `${name}: the engine's format did not reach the open reply`);
    83	  const save = await granted.send('save', { documentHandle: 1, format: p.ms });
    84	  assert.ok(save.call, `${name}: a granted ${p.ms} save did not reach the engine`);
    85	  // Not granted: refused before the engine.
    86	  const plain = sandbox(src, manifestBase, manifestBase.capabilities, p.profile);
    87	  const refusedOpen = await plain.send('open', { buffer: plain.buffer(), name: `report.${p.ms}` });
    88	  assert.equal(refusedOpen.call, undefined, `${name}: an ungranted .${p.ms} open reached the engine`);
    89	  assert.equal(refusedOpen.reply.error.code, 'UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION');
    90	  assert.equal(refusedOpen.reply.error.requiredCapability, `open-${p.ms}`);
    91	  const refusedSave = await plain.send('save', { documentHandle: 1, format: p.ms });
    92	  assert.equal(refusedSave.call, undefined, `${name}: an ungranted ${p.ms} save reached the engine`);
    93	  assert.equal(refusedSave.reply.error.requiredCapability, `save-${p.ms}`);
    94	  // ODF keeps its rows, and a reply without `format` keeps its shape.
    95	  const odfOpen = await plain.send('open', { buffer: plain.buffer(), name: `report.${p.odf}` }, opened(null));
    96	  assert.ok(odfOpen.call, `${name}: the .${p.odf} open was refused`);
    97	  assert.equal(odfOpen.reply.ok, true);
    98	  assert.equal('format' in odfOpen.reply.result, false, `${name}: a format appeared that the engine did not send`);
    99	  const odfSave = await plain.send('save', { documentHandle: 1, format: p.odf });
   100	  assert.ok(odfSave.call, `${name}: the ${p.odf} save was refused`);
   101	}

    36	CALL = "    thisFunctionDoesNotExist();\n"

    39	def compile_commands(target):
    40	    dry = subprocess.run(["make", "-n", "-B", target], capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=SDK)
    41	    joined = re.sub(r"\\\n", " ", dry.stdout)
    42	    commands = []
    43	    for line in joined.splitlines():
    44	        found = re.search(r"\s-c (src/\S+\.cpp) -o (\S+\.o)\s*$", line)
    45	        if found and "em++" in line.split()[0]:
    46	            commands.append({"source": found.group(1), "argv": line.split()})
    47	    if not commands:
    48	        raise SystemExit(f"no compile command found for {target}: {dry.stderr[-400:]}")
    49	    return commands
    50	
    51	
    52	def swap(argv, source, replacement):
    53	    i = argv.index("-c")
    54	    assert argv[i + 1] == source and argv[i + 2] == "-o"
    55	    return argv[:i] + replacement + argv[i + 4:]
    56	
    57	
    58	def run(argv, label, report):
    59	    started = time.monotonic()
    60	    done = subprocess.run(["taskset", "-c", "0", *argv], capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=SDK)
    61	    errors = [line for line in done.stderr.splitlines() if re.search(r"\berror\b", line)]
    62	    report["runs"].append({"label": label, "argv": argv, "exit": done.returncode,
    63	                           "seconds": round(time.monotonic() - started, 1), "errors": errors[:8]})
    64	    print(label, done.returncode, round(time.monotonic() - started, 1), errors[:1], flush=True)
    65	    return done
    66	
    67	
    68	def main():
    69	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    70	    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    71	    args = parser.parse_args()
    72	    out = args.out.resolve()
    73	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    74	    makes = subprocess.run(["pgrep", "-a", "make"], capture_output=True, text=True)
    75	    report = {"uptime": subprocess.check_output(["uptime"], text=True).strip(), "makes": makes.stdout, "runs": [], "exports": {}}
    76	    if makes.returncode == 0:
    77	        (out / "report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=1) + "\n")
    78	        raise SystemExit("a make is running; ask the owner first")
    79	    scratch = out / "scratch"
    80	    shutil.copytree(SDK / "src", scratch / "src")
    81	    for name, source in CONTROL.items():
    82	        path = scratch / "src" / name
    83	        text = path.read_text()
    84	        if text.count(source) != 1:
    85	            raise SystemExit(f"control anchor for {name} occurs {text.count(source)} times")
    86	        path.write_text(text.replace(source, CALL + source))
    87	    verdict = {}
    88	    for profile, spec in PROFILES.items():
    89	        commands = compile_commands(spec["target"])
    90	        report.setdefault("commands", {})[profile] = [c["source"] for c in commands]
    91	        defined = set()
    92	        for c in commands:
    93	            argv, src = c["argv"], c["source"]
    94	            name = Path(src).name
    95	            with_define = "-DOXSDK_MS_FORMATS" in argv
    96	            without = [a for a in argv if a != "-DOXSDK_MS_FORMATS"]
    97	            run(swap(argv, src, ["-fsyntax-only", src]), f"{profile}/syntax/{name}", report)
    98	            if with_define:
    99	                run(swap(without, src, ["-fsyntax-only", src]), f"{profile}/syntax-without-define/{name}", report)
   100	            if name in CONTROL and with_define:
   101	                mutated = str(scratch / "src" / name)
   102	                run(swap(argv, src, ["-fsyntax-only", mutated]), f"{profile}/control-with-define/{name}", report)
   103	                run(swap(without, src, ["-fsyntax-only", mutated]), f"{profile}/control-without-define/{name}", report)
   104	            pre = run(swap(argv, src, ["-E", "-P", src]), f"{profile}/preprocess/{name}", report)
   105	            report["runs"][-1].pop("errors", None)
   106	            defined |= set(re.findall(r"\b(oxsdk_[a-z0-9_]+)\s*\([^;{}]*\)\s*\{", pre.stdout))
   107	        exported = sorted({n.lstrip("_") for n in (SDK / spec["exports"]).read_text().split() if n.startswith("_oxsdk_")})
   108	        missing = [n for n in exported if n not in defined]
   109	        report["exports"][profile] = {"list": spec["exports"], "exported": len(exported), "missing": missing}
   110	        by = {r["label"]: r for r in report["runs"] if r["label"].startswith(profile + "/")}
   111	        controls = [n for n in CONTROL if f"{profile}/control-with-define/{n}" in by]
   112	        verdict[profile] = {
   113	            "syntax-clean": all(r["exit"] == 0 for k, r in by.items() if "/syntax" in k),
   114	            "controls-present": sorted(controls) == sorted(CONTROL),
   115	            "control-caught-with-define": all(by[f"{profile}/control-with-define/{n}"]["exit"] != 0 and
   116	                                              any("thisFunctionDoesNotExist" in e for e in by[f"{profile}/control-with-define/{n}"]["errors"])
   117	                                              for n in controls),
   118	            "control-silent-without-define": all(by[f"{profile}/control-without-define/{n}"]["exit"] == 0 for n in controls),
   119	            "exports-have-bodies": bool(exported) and not missing,
   120	        }
   121	    shutil.rmtree(scratch)
   122	    report["verdict"] = verdict
   123	    (out / "report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=1) + "\n")
   124	    print(json.dumps(verdict, indent=1))
   125	    return 0 if all(all(v.values()) for v in verdict.values()) else 1
   126

   129	    raise SystemExit(main())
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Assemble the Calc undo successor (SPEC-P-calc-undo 4.2) without overwriting a frozen profile.  Derived from build_calc_style_profile.py.

Closed list of differences: the base is ods-editor-v3 and the profile ods-editor-v4; the two undo exports are required; the manifest
gains the `undo` capability (redo rides on it in the worker), bit 8 (OXSDK_CAPABILITY_UNDO) in expectedCapabilityBits, and an
undoContract; this docstring."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BASE = HERE / "dist/profiles/ods-editor-v3"
PROFILE = "ods-editor-v4"
REQUIRED = {"_oxsdk_document_cell", "_oxsdk_document_save", "_oxsdk_document_open",
            "_oxsdk_document_paint", "_oxsdk_document_set_part", "_oxsdk_document_part_info",
            "_oxsdk_document_undo", "_oxsdk_document_redo"}
CAPABILITY_UNDO = 1 << 8  # src/sdk_api.h OXSDK_CAPABILITY_UNDO


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    for name in ("loader", "wasm", "worker", "exports", "output"):
        parser.add_argument("--" + name, type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--development", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    output = args.output.resolve()
    if args.development and not output.is_relative_to((HERE / "build").resolve()):
        raise SystemExit("development assembly must stay under build; frozen profiles are immutable")
    if (output / "sdk-manifest.json").exists() and not args.development:
        raise SystemExit("profile already exists; a frozen profile is not overwritten")
    if output == BASE.resolve():
        raise SystemExit("the predecessor profile must never be overwritten")
    exports = set(args.exports.read_text().split())
    if not REQUIRED <= exports or any(name.startswith("_oxsdk_editor") for name in exports):
        raise SystemExit(f"invalid Calc exports: missing {sorted(REQUIRED - exports)} or Writer API present")
    manifest = json.loads((BASE / "sdk-manifest.json").read_text())
    if "undo" in manifest["capabilities"] or manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"] & CAPABILITY_UNDO:
        raise SystemExit("the base already claims undo")
    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for name, source in (("probe.js", args.loader), ("probe.wasm", args.wasm), ("sdk-worker.js", args.worker)):
        shutil.copyfile(source, output / name)
    for key in ("soffice.data", "soffice.data.js.metadata"):
        name = manifest["artifactFiles"][key].removeprefix("./")
        shutil.copyfile(BASE / name, output / name)
    manifest["profile"] = PROFILE
    manifest["sdkVersion"] = "0.6.0-r6-worker+" + PROFILE
    manifest["capabilities"].append("undo")
    manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"] |= CAPABILITY_UNDO
    manifest["undoContract"] = {
        "version": 1, "operations": ["undo", "redo"],
        "emptyStack": "NOTHING_TO_UNDO / NOTHING_TO_REDO before dispatch",
        "proof": "the undo and redo lists move by exactly one, else MUTATION_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN",
        "reply": ["revision", "undoCount", "redoCount", "cursor {part, column, row, moved} or null"],
        "singleCellBoldItalic": "one undo action (one setPropertyValues)",
    }
    (output / "sdk-manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps({"profile": PROFILE, "development": args.development,
        "capabilityBits": manifest["expectedCapabilityBits"],
        "sha256": {name: hashlib.sha256((output / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                   for name in ("probe.js", "probe.wasm", "sdk-worker.js", "sdk-manifest.json")}}, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# The a11y successor, roadmap 3.4.  Its own build and dist directories for the
# same reason every predecessor got them: v4 must stay byte-identical, because
# the E2-C verdict and every scrap of round-two evidence bind to it.
#
# 153: one long paragraph overflows the engine thread's 64 KiB stack.  Every
# product link from v22 calls this macro.  link_r5_product stays, byte-unchanged,
# for the frozen and diagnostic rules that already call it (spec 4.1: a new macro
# rather than a parameter, so nothing about those 22 callers needs proving).  That
# this is otherwise a faithful copy is criterion S3: make -n against v21's differs
# in the link line by that one token.  STACK_OVERFLOW_CHECK stays 0 at ASSERTIONS=0
# (L-153-no-overflow-check, spec 3.3).
define link_product_stack_1m
	$(CXX) $(1) \
		-L$(LOBUILD)/instdir/program $$(cat $(LINKDEPS)) \
		-pthread -fwasm-exceptions -sSUPPORT_LONGJMP=wasm --no-entry -Oz \
		-sMODULARIZE -sEXPORT_NAME=createProbeModule \
		-sTOTAL_MEMORY=1GB -sPTHREAD_POOL_SIZE=7 -sPTHREAD_POOL_SIZE_STRICT=0 \
		-sSTACK_SIZE=131072 -sDEFAULT_PTHREAD_STACK_SIZE=1048576 \
		-sFORCE_FILESYSTEM=1 -sWASM_BIGINT=1 -sERROR_ON_UNDEFINED_SYMBOLS=1 -sFETCH=1 \
		-sASSERTIONS=0 -sEXIT_RUNTIME=0 \
		-sEXPORTED_RUNTIME_METHODS=ccall,FS,HEAPU8 \
		-sEXPORTED_FUNCTIONS=@$(2) \
		-Wl,--wrap=_Z18initJsUnoScriptingv \
		--pre-js $(LOSRC)/static/emscripten/environment.js \
		--pre-js $(FS_PRE_JS) \
		-o $(3)
endef

# Finding 037 / 032.  The stuck thread is parked, not spinning (measured: a CPU
# profile during the hang is identical to one taken before it), so profiling is
# exhausted and the question is what it is waiting for.  Emscripten already
# knows how to answer that -- PTHREADS_DEBUG is a link-time setting that traces
# futex waits, mailbox activity and calls proxied to the main thread, which are
# exactly the three ways a thread can park here.
#
# Link-level only: no core change, no engine source change, and its own build
# and dist directories.  The shipped e2-format-discovery must not be rebuilt --
# A3/A4/A5 are bound to c89f069e and a rebuild unbinds all three (finding 027,
# finding 036).
#
# --profiling-funcs keeps the function names, so the trace names functions
# instead of wasm-function[36295].  ASSERTIONS=1 turns on a second batch of
# pthread checks that share the same #if.
# Relinking a frozen profile is never incidental.  The wasm hash is not a
# function of the source (finding 036) and every verdict binds to the artifact
# it was measured on (finding 027), so a new link silently invalidates whatever
# that profile carries.  The way it happens is never "someone decided to": it is
# a Makefile edit for an unrelated profile making these targets out of date.
#
#   $(1)  what the profile carries, for the message
define refuse_unasked_relink
	@if [ "$(ALLOW_FROZEN_RELINK)" != "1" ]; then \
		echo ""; \
		echo "refusing to relink a frozen profile: $@"; \
		echo "it carries: $(1)"; \
		echo "a new link mints a new artifact and unbinds that (findings 027, 036)."; \
		echo "if that is really what you want:  make ALLOW_FROZEN_RELINK=1 $@"; \
		echo ""; \
		exit 1; \
	fi
endef

    41	int32_t status(probe::SubmitStatus value) {
    42	  return static_cast<int32_t>(value);

    44	
    45	template <typename Function> int32_t safeStatus(Function &&function) noexcept {
    46	  try {
    47	    return status(function());
    48	  } catch (...) {
    49	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INTERNAL_ERROR;
    50	  }
    51	}
    52	
    53	bool compatibleVersion(std::uint32_t requested) {
    54	  const std::uint32_t major = requested >> 16;
    55	  const std::uint32_t minor = requested & 0xffffu;
    56	  return major == OXSDK_ABI_VERSION_MAJOR && minor <= OXSDK_ABI_VERSION_MINOR;
    57	}
    58	} // namespace
    59	
    60	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE std::uint32_t oxsdk_abi_version(void) {
    61	  return OXSDK_ABI_VERSION;
    62	}
    63	
    64	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE std::uint32_t oxsdk_capabilities(void) {
    65	#if defined(OXSDK_ODS_VIEWER_V2)
    66	  // SPEC-M4-viewer-v2 3.1: v1's bits plus part_info, plus form-controls when
    67	  // the core has the forms components.  The manifest's expectedCapabilityBits
    68	  // must equal this, so a manifest cannot claim forms this core lacks.
    69	  return OXSDK_CAPABILITY_OPEN_ODT | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_RGBA_TILE |
    70	         OXSDK_CAPABILITY_CANCEL_QUEUED | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_SEARCH |
    71	         OXSDK_CAPABILITY_OPEN_ODS | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_PART_SWITCH |
    72	         OXSDK_CAPABILITY_PART_TEXT | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_PART_INFO
    73	#ifdef OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR
    74	         | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_CELL_EDITING | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_SAVE_ODS
    75	#ifdef OXSDK_ODS_RANGE
    76	         | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_CELL_RANGE_FORMATTING
    77	#endif
    78	#ifdef OXSDK_ODS_STYLE
    79	         | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_CELL_RANGE_STYLING
    80	#endif
    81	#ifdef OXSDK_ODS_UNDO
    82	         // SPEC-P-calc-undo 4.2 E1: the engine's undo walk (probe_engine.cpp,
    83	         // startOdsUndoWalk) and the two exports; redo rides on this bit.
    84	         | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_UNDO
    85	#endif
    86	#ifdef OXSDK_MS_FORMATS
    87	         | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_OPEN_XLSX | OXSDK_CAPABILITY_SAVE_XLSX
    88	#endif

   119	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_engine_start(
   120	    std::uint32_t requestedAbiVersion, oxsdk_request_id requestId) {
   121	  if (!validRequest(requestId))
   122	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
   123	  if (!compatibleVersion(requestedAbiVersion))
   124	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INCOMPATIBLE_ABI;
   125	  return safeStatus([&] { return probe::start(requestId); });
   126	}
   127	
   128	extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_open(
   129	    oxsdk_request_id requestId, const std::uint8_t *bytes,
   130	    std::uint32_t byteLength, const char *nameUtf8, std::uint32_t nameLength) {
   131	  if (!validRequest(requestId) || !bytes || byteLength == 0 || !nameUtf8 ||
   132	      nameLength == 0 || nameLength > 255) {
   133	    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  return safeStatus([&] {
   137	    std::string name(nameUtf8, nameLength);
   138	#ifdef OXSDK_MS_FORMATS
   139	    // SPEC-P-ms-formats 5.1 E1 (finding 013's narrow surface): the table, per build.
   140	    if (probe::docformat::acceptedSuffix(probe::docformat::kThisBuild, name).empty())
   141	      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
   142	#elif defined(OXSDK_ODS_VIEWER)
   143	    // W7a (finding 013's allowlist): `.ods` beside `.odt`, either case.
   144	    // Content detection still reads the zip's own `mimetype`.
   145	    if (probe::odsviewer::acceptedSuffix(name).empty())
   146	      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
   147	#else
   148	    if (name.size() < 4 || name.substr(name.size() - 4) != ".odt")
   149	      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
   150	#endif
   151	    return probe::openBytes(
   152	        requestId, std::vector<std::uint8_t>(bytes, bytes + byteLength),
   153	        std::move(name));
   154	  });
   155	}

def wait(session, expression, timeout=120):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    last = None
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        last = evaluate(session, expression)
        if last:
            return last
        time.sleep(.1)
    raise RuntimeError(f"condition did not become true: {expression}; last {last}")


def key(session, name):
    codes = {"Enter": (13, "\ue007"), "Escape": (27, "\ue00c"), "Backspace": (8, "\ue003")}
    code, webdriver = codes[name]
    if isinstance(session, ChromeSession):
        session.call("Input.dispatchKeyEvent", {"type": "keyDown", "key": name, "code": name, "windowsVirtualKeyCode": code})
        session.call("Input.dispatchKeyEvent", {"type": "keyUp", "key": name, "code": name, "windowsVirtualKeyCode": code})
    else:
        element = session.command("GET", "/element/active")["element-6066-11e4-a52e-4f735466cecf"]
        session.command("POST", f"/element/{element}/value", {"text": webdriver})


def type_text(session, selector, text):
    wait(session, f"!document.querySelector({json.dumps(selector)}).disabled")
    evaluate(session, f"(()=>{{const n=document.querySelector({json.dumps(selector)});n.focus();n.select();return true;}})()")
    key(session, "Backspace")
    if text:
        if isinstance(session, ChromeSession):
            session.call("Input.insertText", {"text": text})
        else:
            element = session.command("GET", "/element/active")["element-6066-11e4-a52e-4f735466cecf"]
            session.command("POST", f"/element/{element}/value", {"text": text})


def select(session, address):
    type_text(session, "#cell-address", address)
    key(session, "Enter")
    wait(session, f"window.__calcEditor.state.cell && !window.__calcEditor.state.busy && document.querySelector('#cell-address').value === {json.dumps(address)}")


def double_click(session, x, y):
    if isinstance(session, ChromeSession):
        for count in (1, 2):
            for kind in ("mousePressed", "mouseReleased"):
                session.call("Input.dispatchMouseEvent", {"type": kind, "x": x, "y": y, "button": "left", "clickCount": count})
            time.sleep(.08)
    else:
        session.command("POST", "/actions", {"actions": [{"type": "pointer", "id": "mouse", "parameters": {"pointerType": "mouse"}, "actions": [
            {"type": "pointerMove", "duration": 0, "origin": "viewport", "x": round(x), "y": round(y)},
            {"type": "pointerDown", "button": 0}, {"type": "pointerUp", "button": 0}, {"type": "pause", "duration": 80},
            {"type": "pointerDown", "button": 0}, {"type": "pointerUp", "button": 0}]}]})


def exercise(session, out, run):
    def check(name, passed, actual):
        run["checks"].append({"id": name, "pass": bool(passed), "actual": actual})
        if not passed:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{name} failed: {actual!r}")

    def state():
        return evaluate(session, "window.__calcEditor.state")

    def commit_formula(address, text):
        select(session, address)
        type_text(session, "#formula-input", text)
        key(session, "Enter")
        wait(session, "!window.__calcEditor.state.busy && !window.__calcEditor.state.editing")
        snapshot = state()
        check("formula-input/" + address, snapshot["cell"]["input"] == text, snapshot["cell"])
        return snapshot

    prefix = f"{run['browser']}-r{run['repeat']}"
    wait(session, "document.querySelectorAll('#tiles canvas').length > 0 && !window.__calcEditor.state.busy")
    point = evaluate(session, "(()=>{const b=document.querySelector('#surface').getBoundingClientRect();return {x:b.left+40,y:b.top+9};})()")
    double_click(session, point["x"], point["y"])
    wait(session, "window.__calcEditor.state.editing === 'inline'")
}

static int doc_saveAs(LibreOfficeKitDocument* pThis, const char* sUrl, const char* pFormat, const char* pFilterOptions)
{
    comphelper::ProfileZone aZone("doc_saveAs");

    SolarMutexGuard aGuard;
    SetLastExceptionMsg();

    LibLODocument_Impl* pDocument = static_cast<LibLODocument_Impl*>(pThis);

    OUString sFormat = getUString(pFormat);
    OUString aURL(getAbsoluteURL(sUrl));

    uno::Reference<frame::XStorable> xStorable(pDocument->mxComponent, uno::UNO_QUERY_THROW);

    if (aURL.isEmpty())
    {
        SetLastExceptionMsg(u"Filename to save to was not provided."_ustr);
        SAL_INFO("lok", "URL for save is empty");
        return false;
    }

    try
    {
        std::span<const ExtensionMap> pMap;

        switch (doc_getDocumentType(pThis))
        {
        case LOK_DOCTYPE_SPREADSHEET:
            pMap = aCalcExtensionMap;
            break;
        case LOK_DOCTYPE_PRESENTATION:
            pMap = aImpressExtensionMap;
            break;
        case LOK_DOCTYPE_DRAWING:
            pMap = aDrawExtensionMap;
            break;
        case LOK_DOCTYPE_TEXT:
            pMap = aWriterExtensionMap;
            break;
        case LOK_DOCTYPE_OTHER:
        default:
            SAL_INFO("lok", "Can't save document - unsupported document type.");
            return false;
        }

        if (pFormat == nullptr)
        {
            // sniff from the extension
            sal_Int32 idx = aURL.lastIndexOf(".");
            if( idx > 0 )
            {
                sFormat = aURL.copy( idx + 1 );
            }
            else
            {
                SetLastExceptionMsg("input URL '" + aURL + "' lacks a suffix");
                return false;
            }
        }

        OUString aFilterName;
        for (const auto& item : pMap)
        {
            if (sFormat.equalsIgnoreAsciiCaseAscii(item.extn))
            {
                aFilterName = item.filterName;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (aFilterName.isEmpty())
        {
            SetLastExceptionMsg(u"no output filter found for provided suffix"_ustr);
            return false;
        }

        OUString aFilterOptions = getUString(pFilterOptions);

        // Check if watermark for pdf is passed by filteroptions...
        // It is not a real filter option so it must be filtered out.
        OUString watermarkText;
        std::u16string_view sFullSheetPreview;
        int aIndex = -1;
        if ((aIndex = aFilterOptions.indexOf(",Watermark=")) >= 0)
        {
            int bIndex = aFilterOptions.indexOf("WATERMARKEND");
            watermarkText = aFilterOptions.subView(aIndex+11, bIndex-(aIndex+11));
            aFilterOptions = OUString::Concat(aFilterOptions.subView(0, aIndex)) + aFilterOptions.subView(bIndex+12);
        }

        if ((aIndex = aFilterOptions.indexOf(",FullSheetPreview=")) >= 0)
        {
            int bIndex = aFilterOptions.indexOf("FULLSHEETPREVEND");
            sFullSheetPreview = aFilterOptions.subView(aIndex+18, bIndex-(aIndex+18));
            aFilterOptions = OUString::Concat(aFilterOptions.subView(0, aIndex)) + aFilterOptions.subView(bIndex+16);
        }

        bool bFullSheetPreview = sFullSheetPreview == u"true";

        OUString filePassword;
        if ((aIndex = aFilterOptions.indexOf(",Password=")) >= 0)
        {
            int bIndex = aFilterOptions.indexOf("PASSWORDEND");
            filePassword = aFilterOptions.subView(aIndex + 10, bIndex - (aIndex + 10));
            aFilterOptions = OUString::Concat(aFilterOptions.subView(0, aIndex))
                             + aFilterOptions.subView(bIndex + 11);
        }
        OUString filePasswordToModify;
        if ((aIndex = aFilterOptions.indexOf(",PasswordToModify=")) >= 0)
        {
            int bIndex = aFilterOptions.indexOf("PASSWORDTOMODIFYEND");
            filePassword = aFilterOptions.subView(aIndex + 18, bIndex - (aIndex + 18));
            aFilterOptions = OUString::Concat(aFilterOptions.subView(0, aIndex))
                             + aFilterOptions.subView(bIndex + 19);
        }

        // Select a pdf version if specified a valid one. If not specified then ignore.
        // If invalid then fail.
        sal_Int32 pdfVer = 0;
        if ((aIndex = aFilterOptions.indexOf(",PDFVer=")) >= 0)
        {
            int bIndex = aFilterOptions.indexOf("PDFVEREND");
            std::u16string_view sPdfVer = aFilterOptions.subView(aIndex+8, bIndex-(aIndex+8));
            aFilterOptions = OUString::Concat(aFilterOptions.subView(0, aIndex)) + aFilterOptions.subView(bIndex+9);

            if (o3tl::equalsIgnoreAsciiCase(sPdfVer, u"PDF/A-1b"))
                pdfVer = 1;
            else if (o3tl::equalsIgnoreAsciiCase(sPdfVer, u"PDF/A-2b"))
                pdfVer = 2;
            else if (o3tl::equalsIgnoreAsciiCase(sPdfVer, u"PDF/A-3b"))
                pdfVer = 3;
            else if (o3tl::equalsIgnoreAsciiCase(sPdfVer, u"PDF/A-4"))
                pdfVer = 4;
            else if (o3tl::equalsIgnoreAsciiCase(sPdfVer, u"PDF-1.5"))
                pdfVer = 15;
            else if (o3tl::equalsIgnoreAsciiCase(sPdfVer, u"PDF-1.6"))
                pdfVer = 16;
            else if (o3tl::equalsIgnoreAsciiCase(sPdfVer, u"PDF-1.7"))
                pdfVer = 17;
            else if (o3tl::equalsIgnoreAsciiCase(sPdfVer, u"PDF-2.0"))
                pdfVer = 20;
            else
            {
                SetLastExceptionMsg(u"wrong PDF version"_ustr);
                return false;
            }
        }

        // 'TakeOwnership' == this is a 'real' SaveAs (that is, the document
        // gets a new name).  When this is not provided, the meaning of
        // saveAs() is more like save-a-copy, which allows saving to any
        // random format like PDF or PNG.
        // It is not a real filter option, so we have to filter it out.
        const uno::Sequence<OUString> aOptionSeq = comphelper::string::convertCommaSeparated(aFilterOptions);
        std::vector<OUString> aFilteredOptionVec;
        bool bTakeOwnership = false;
        bool bCreateFromTemplate = false;
        comphelper::SequenceAsHashMap aSaveMediaDescriptor;
        for (const auto& rOption : aOptionSeq)
        {
            if (rOption == "TakeOwnership")
                bTakeOwnership = true;
            else if (rOption == "NoFileSync")
                aSaveMediaDescriptor[u"NoFileSync"_ustr] <<= true;
            else if (rOption == "FromTemplate")
                bCreateFromTemplate = true;
            else
                aFilteredOptionVec.push_back(rOption);
        }

        if (bCreateFromTemplate && bTakeOwnership)
        {
            if (SfxObjectShell* pObjectShell = getSfxObjectShell(pThis))
            {
                DateTime now( ::DateTime::SYSTEM );
                pObjectShell->getDocProperties()->setCreationDate(now.GetUNODateTime());
            }
        }

        aSaveMediaDescriptor[u"Overwrite"_ustr] <<= true;
        aSaveMediaDescriptor[u"FilterName"_ustr] <<= aFilterName;

        auto aFilteredOptionSeq = comphelper::containerToSequence<OUString>(aFilteredOptionVec);
        aFilterOptions = comphelper::string::convertCommaSeparated(aFilteredOptionSeq);
        aSaveMediaDescriptor[MediaDescriptor::PROP_FILTEROPTIONS] <<= aFilterOptions;

extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_engine_start(
    std::uint32_t requestedAbiVersion, oxsdk_request_id requestId) {
  if (!validRequest(requestId))
    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
  if (!compatibleVersion(requestedAbiVersion))
    return OXSDK_STATUS_INCOMPATIBLE_ABI;
  return safeStatus([&] { return probe::start(requestId); });
}

extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_open(
    oxsdk_request_id requestId, const std::uint8_t *bytes,
    std::uint32_t byteLength, const char *nameUtf8, std::uint32_t nameLength) {
  if (!validRequest(requestId) || !bytes || byteLength == 0 || !nameUtf8 ||
      nameLength == 0 || nameLength > 255) {
    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
  }

  return safeStatus([&] {
    std::string name(nameUtf8, nameLength);
#ifdef OXSDK_MS_FORMATS
    // SPEC-P-ms-formats 5.1 E1 (finding 013's narrow surface): the table, per build.
    if (probe::docformat::acceptedSuffix(probe::docformat::kThisBuild, name).empty())
      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
#elif defined(OXSDK_ODS_VIEWER)
    // W7a (finding 013's allowlist): `.ods` beside `.odt`, either case.
    // Content detection still reads the zip's own `mimetype`.
    if (probe::odsviewer::acceptedSuffix(name).empty())
      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
#else
    if (name.size() < 4 || name.substr(name.size() - 4) != ".odt")
      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
#endif
    return probe::openBytes(
        requestId, std::vector<std::uint8_t>(bytes, bytes + byteLength),
        std::move(name));
  });
}

extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_paint(
    oxsdk_request_id requestId, oxsdk_document_handle documentHandle,
    std::int32_t xTwips, std::int32_t yTwips, std::int32_t widthTwips,
    std::int32_t heightTwips, std::int32_t canvasWidthPx,
     1	// specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 6.1 F1: src/document_format.hpp, every cell of table E1, native, no core.
     2	// Red cases: tests/document_format_mutations.py (generated from the header's table).
     3	//   g++ -std=c++17 -I src tests/document_format_test.cpp -o /tmp/t && /tmp/t
     4	#include "document_format.hpp"
     5	
     6	#include <cstdio>
     7	#include <string>
     8	#include <vector>
     9	
    10	namespace df = probe::docformat;
    11	
    12	namespace {
    13	int failures = 0;
    14	
    15	void check(bool ok, const std::string &what) {
    16	  if (!ok) {
    17	    ++failures;
    18	    std::printf("FAIL %s\n", what.c_str());
    19	  }
    20	}
    21	
    22	struct Expected {
    23	  std::string suffix, format;
    24	  bool savable;
    25	};
    26	
    27	// Table E1 as the spec writes it.  The header must equal this exactly: an entry added there and not
    28	// here is as red as one missing.
    29	const std::vector<Expected> kWriter = {{".odt", "odt", true}, {".docx", "docx", true}};
    30	const std::vector<Expected> kCalc = {{".odt", "odt", false}, {".ods", "ods", true}, {".xlsx", "xlsx", true}};
    31	
    32	// Names no profile accepts (the spec's refusals and the shapes around a suffix).
    33	const std::vector<std::string> kRefusedEverywhere = {
    34	    "a.doc", "a.xls", "a.docm", "a.xlsm", "a.dotx", "a.xltx", "a.rtf", "a.csv", "a.pdf", "a.ott",
    35	    "", "docx", "odt", "a.docx.zip", "a.ods.bak", "a.od", "a.xlsx ", " "};
    36	
    37	void profile(df::Profile which, const char *label, const std::vector<Expected> &expected,
    38	             const std::vector<std::string> &refusedHere) {
    39	  const std::string p = label;
    40	  std::size_t count = 0;
    41	  const df::Entry *entries = df::table(which, count);
    42	  // T1 the table is exactly the spec's.
    43	  check(count == expected.size(), "T1 " + p + " has " + std::to_string(expected.size()) + " entries");
    44	  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < expected.size() && i < count; ++i) {
    45	    const auto &e = expected[i];
    46	    check(entries[i].suffix == e.suffix && entries[i].format == e.format && entries[i].savable == e.savable,
    47	          "T1 " + p + " entry " + std::to_string(i) + " is " + e.suffix);
    48	  }
    49	  for (const auto &e : expected) {
    50	    const std::string stem = "Report";
    51	    // A1 accepted under its own suffix, either case, with a one-character stem.
    52	    check(df::acceptedSuffix(which, stem + e.suffix) == e.suffix, "A1 " + p + " accepts report" + e.suffix);
    53	    check(df::acceptedSuffix(which, df::lower(stem) + df::lower(e.suffix)) == e.suffix, "A1 " + p + " lower case " + e.suffix);
    54	    std::string upper = e.suffix;
    55	    for (char &c : upper) c = static_cast<char>(std::toupper(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
    56	    check(df::acceptedSuffix(which, "REPORT" + upper) == e.suffix, "A1 " + p + " upper case " + e.suffix);
    57	    check(df::acceptedSuffix(which, "a" + e.suffix) == e.suffix, "A1 " + p + " one-character stem " + e.suffix);
    58	    check(df::acceptedSuffix(which, "我的文件" + e.suffix) == e.suffix, "A1 " + p + " a CJK stem " + e.suffix);
    59	    // R1 the suffix alone is not a name.
    60	    check(df::acceptedSuffix(which, e.suffix).empty(), "R1 " + p + " refuses the bare " + e.suffix);
    61	    // O1 the open reply's format.
    62	    check(df::openFormat(which, stem + e.suffix) == e.format, "O1 " + p + " reports " + e.format);
    63	    // S1 save follows the table, as written (the worker sends lower case).
    64	    check(df::savable(which, e.format) == e.savable, "S1 " + p + " save " + e.format);
    65	    std::string upperFormat = e.format;
    66	    for (char &c : upperFormat) c = static_cast<char>(std::toupper(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
    67	    check(!df::savable(which, upperFormat), "S1 " + p + " save is compared as written: " + upperFormat);
    68	  }
    69	  for (const auto &name : kRefusedEverywhere) {
    70	    check(df::acceptedSuffix(which, name).empty(), "R1 " + p + " refuses '" + name + "'");
    71	    check(df::openFormat(which, name).empty(), "O1 " + p + " reports nothing for '" + name + "'");
    72	  }
    73	  for (const auto &name : refusedHere)
    74	    check(df::acceptedSuffix(which, name).empty(), "R1 " + p + " refuses '" + name + "'");
    75	  for (const char *format : {"doc", "xls", "docm", "xlsm", "rtf", "csv", "pdf", "", "ods.", "odt "})
    76	    check(!df::savable(which, format), "S1 " + p + " refuses to save '" + std::string(format) + "'");
    77	}
    78	} // namespace
    79	
    80	int main() {
    81	  profile(df::Profile::Writer, "writer", kWriter, {"a.ods", "a.xlsx"});
    82	  profile(df::Profile::Calc, "calc", kCalc, {"a.docx"});
    83	  check(!df::savable(df::Profile::Writer, "ods") && !df::savable(df::Profile::Writer, "xlsx"),
    84	        "S1 writer does not save spreadsheet formats");
    85	  check(!df::savable(df::Profile::Calc, "docx"), "S1 calc does not save docx");
    86	  if (failures) {
    87	    std::printf("%d failure(s)\n", failures);
    88	    return 1;
    89	  }
    90	  std::printf("ok\n");
    91	  return 0;
    92	}
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""The red cases of tests/document_format_test.cpp against src/document_format.hpp (specs/SPEC-P-ms-formats.md 6.1 F1).
     3	
     4	The mutations are GENERATED from the header's table, not listed by hand: every entry of kWriter and kCalc gets a
     5	`drop` (the line removed) and a `flip` (its savable inverted), and every table gets an `add` (a `.doc` entry appended),
     6	so an entry added to the header later gets its red cases without anyone editing this file.  Six rule mutations are
     7	written out below.  The test is built once against the header as it is (the control, which must pass) and once per
     8	mutation, each of which must fail in the named check family.  Each edit must match exactly once; a table with no
     9	entries is refused (a census of nothing is not a pass).
    10	
    11	Usage: python3 -I tests/document_format_mutations.py [--cxx g++]
    12	Exit 0 only when the control passes and every mutation is red as required.
    13	"""
    14	import argparse
    15	import pathlib
    16	import re
    17	import shutil
    18	import subprocess
    19	import sys
    20	import tempfile
    21	
    22	HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    23	HEADER = HERE / "src" / "document_format.hpp"
    24	TEST = HERE / "tests" / "document_format_test.cpp"
    25	
    26	RULES = {
    27	    "case-sensitive": (
    28	        "  for (char &c : text)\n    c = static_cast<char>(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));\n", "", "A1"),
    29	    "no-stem-required": ("lowered.size() > suffix.size() &&", "lowered.size() >= suffix.size() &&", "R1"),
    30	    "suffix-anywhere": (
    31	        "lowered.compare(lowered.size() - suffix.size(), suffix.size(), suffix) == 0)",
    32	        "lowered.find(suffix) != std::string::npos)", "R1"),
    33	    "profiles-swapped": ("  if (profile == Profile::Calc) {", "  if (profile != Profile::Calc) {", "T1"),
    34	    "save-ignores-savable": ("if (entries[i].savable && format == entries[i].format)",
    35	                             "if (format == entries[i].format)", "S1"),
    36	    "save-any-case": ("if (entries[i].savable && format == entries[i].format)",
    37	                      "if (entries[i].savable && lower(format) == entries[i].format)", "S1"),
    38	}
    39	
    40	
    41	def table_mutations(text):
    42	    out = {}
    43	    for table in ("kWriter", "kCalc"):
    44	        block = re.search(r"inline constexpr Entry %s\[\] = \{\n(.*?)\n\};" % table, text, re.S)
    45	        if not block:
    46	            raise SystemExit(f"{table} not found in the header")
    47	        lines = [l for l in block.group(1).split("\n") if l.strip().startswith("{")]
    48	        if not lines:
    49	            raise SystemExit(f"{table} has no entries")
    50	        for line in lines:
    51	            suffix = re.search(r'\{"([^"]+)"', line).group(1)
    52	            out[f"{table}-drop{suffix}"] = (line + "\n", "", "T1")
    53	            flipped = line.replace("true}", "FLIP}").replace("false}", "true}").replace("FLIP}", "false}")
    54	            out[f"{table}-flip{suffix}"] = (line + "\n", flipped + "\n", "T1")
    55	        last = lines[-1]
    56	        out[f"{table}-add.doc"] = (last + "\n", last + "\n" + '    {".doc", "doc", true},\n', "T1")
    57	    return out
    58	
    59	
    60	def build_and_run(cxx, header_text, work):
    61	    src = work / "src"
    62	    src.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    63	    (src / "document_format.hpp").write_text(header_text)
    64	    exe = work / "t"
    65	    compiled = subprocess.run([cxx, "-std=c++17", "-I", str(src), str(TEST), "-o", str(exe)], capture_output=True, text=True)
    66	    if compiled.returncode:
    67	        return "compile", compiled.stderr[-600:]
    68	    ran = subprocess.run([str(exe)], capture_output=True, text=True)
    69	    return ran.returncode, ran.stdout
    70	
    71	
    72	def main():
    73	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    74	    parser.add_argument("--cxx", default="g++")
    75	    args = parser.parse_args()
    76	    text = HEADER.read_text()
    77	    mutations = {**table_mutations(text), **RULES}
    78	    results, ok = {}, True
    79	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
    80	        work = pathlib.Path(tmp)
    81	        code, output = build_and_run(args.cxx, text, work)
    82	        control = code == 0 and output.strip().endswith("ok")
    83	        results["control"] = {"exit": code, "pass": control}
    84	        ok &= control
    85	        for name, (old, new, family) in mutations.items():
    86	            if text.count(old) != 1:
    87	                results[name] = {"red": False, "why": f"anchor matches {text.count(old)} times"}
    88	                ok = False
    89	                continue
    90	            code, output = build_and_run(args.cxx, text.replace(old, new), work)
    91	            red = code not in (0, "compile") and f"FAIL {family} " in output
    92	            results[name] = {"red": red, "exit": code, "family": family,
    93	                             "firstFailure": next((l for l in str(output).splitlines() if l.startswith("FAIL")), None)}
    94	            ok &= red
    95	    for name, r in results.items():
    96	        print(f"{name}: {r}")
    97	    print(f"{len(mutations)} mutations; {'ALL RED, control green' if ok else 'NOT ALL RED or control red'}")
    98	    return 0 if ok else 1
    99	
   100	
   101	if __name__ == "__main__":
   102	    sys.exit(main())

// SPEC-P-demo-batch-3 3.1: the closed spellings the bridge accepts.
function styleValueIsClosed(style, value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string") return false;
  if (style === "textColor") return value === "auto" || /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/.test(value);
  if (style === "backColor") return value === "none" || /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/.test(value);
  return style === "horizontalAlign"
    && ["standard", "left", "center", "right", "block"].includes(value);
}

def mirror(out, profile):
    root = PROJECT / "build/calc-editor-browser" / out.name
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    for name in ("ods-editor.html", "ods-editor-app.js"):
        shutil.copyfile(PROJECT / "web" / name, root / name)
    # This generic mirror deliberately aliases its selected engine as v1.
    # Keep that private URL valid after the product switches to a successor;
    # successor-specific wrappers rename both the alias and URL together.
    app=root/"ods-editor-app.js"
    source=app.read_text()
    # ods-editor-v4 added 2026-11-03 when the page moved to it (SPEC-P-calc-undo); a page URL this list lacks loads nothing.
    for live in ('"./profiles/ods-editor-v2/sdk-worker.js"','"./profiles/ods-editor-v3/sdk-worker.js"','"./profiles/ods-editor-v4/sdk-worker.js"'):
        source=source.replace(live,'"./profiles/ods-editor-v1/sdk-worker.js"')
    app.write_text(source)
    for directory in ("sdk", "viewer-shell", "spreadsheet-shell"):
        shutil.copytree(PROJECT / directory, root / directory)
    (root / "reader-shell").mkdir()
    shutil.copyfile(PROJECT / "reader-shell/tile-scheduler.js", root / "reader-shell/tile-scheduler.js")
    (root / "profiles").mkdir()
    (root / "profiles/ods-editor-v1").symlink_to(profile.resolve(), target_is_directory=True)
    (root / "profiles/resources").symlink_to(PROJECT / "dist/profiles/resources", target_is_directory=True)
    hashes = {str(p.relative_to(root)): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
              for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}

  async listTrackedChanges(options = {}) {
    this._assertUsable();
    const result = await this._engine._request("listTrackedChanges", {
      documentHandle: this.handle,
    }, options);
    this.revision = result.revision;
    return result;
  }

  async save(options = {}, requestOptions = {}) {
    this._assertUsable();
    const format = options.format || "odt";
    const result = await this._engine._request("save", {
      documentHandle: this.handle,
      format,
    }, requestOptions);
    this.revision = result.revision;
    return result.buffer;
  }

  async close(options = {}) {
    if (this._closed)
      return;
    this._assertUsable();
    const requestedTimeout = Number(options.timeoutMs ?? this._engine._defaultTimeoutMs);
    const timeoutMs = Number.isFinite(requestedTimeout) && requestedTimeout > 0
      ? Math.min(requestedTimeout, this._engine._closeRecoveryTimeoutMs)
      : this._engine._closeRecoveryTimeoutMs;
    try {
      await this._engine._request("close", {
        documentHandle: this.handle,
      }, { ...options, timeoutMs });
    } catch (error) {
      if (!(error instanceof SdkTimeoutError))
        throw error;
      await this._engine._recoverTimedOutClose(this.handle, timeoutMs);
    }
    this._closed = true;
  }
}

void open(std::string fileUrl) {
  Command command{CommandType::OpenUrl};
  command.text = std::move(fileUrl);
  (void)submit(std::move(command));
}

SubmitStatus openBytes(std::uint32_t requestId, std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes,
                       std::string name) {
  Command command{CommandType::OpenBytes};
  command.sdk = true;
  command.requestId = requestId;
  command.name = std::move(name);
  command.bytes = std::move(bytes);
  return submit(std::move(command));
}

  return submit(std::move(command));
}

void close() { (void)submit({CommandType::Close}); }

codex
已確認後續預定使用的 mirror 會提供 `profiles/resources` 別名，因此資源相對路徑不算該流程的啟動阻礙。另外找到兩個問題：Writer manifest 仍會宣告 v32 的三個雜湊；save 閘門比較原始值，傳給 ABI 前卻轉成字串，可能讓非字串值繞過格式專用 capability。正在做不寫檔的最小驗證。

extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_insert_text(
    oxsdk_request_id requestId, oxsdk_document_handle documentHandle,
    const char *utf8, std::uint32_t utf8Length) {
  if (!validRequest(requestId) || documentHandle == 0 || !utf8 ||
      utf8Length == 0)
    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
  return safeStatus([&] {
    return probe::insertText(requestId, documentHandle,
                             std::string(utf8, utf8Length));
  });
}
#endif

// The viewer has no save (SPEC-M4 3.2, review M2): not in its export list and
// not compiled, because EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE exports a function whatever the
// list says -- the reader's click is kept out the same way.
#if !defined(OXSDK_ODS_VIEWER) || defined(OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR)
extern "C" EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int32_t oxsdk_document_save(
    oxsdk_request_id requestId, oxsdk_document_handle documentHandle,
    const char *formatUtf8, std::uint32_t formatLength) {
  if (!validRequest(requestId) || documentHandle == 0 || !formatUtf8 ||
      formatLength == 0)
    return OXSDK_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT;
  return safeStatus([&] {
    const std::string format(formatUtf8, formatLength);
#ifdef OXSDK_MS_FORMATS
    if (!probe::docformat::savable(probe::docformat::kThisBuild, format))
#elif defined(OXSDK_ODS_EDITOR)
    if (format != "ods")
#else
    if (format != "odt")
#endif
      return probe::SubmitStatus::InvalidArgument;
    return probe::save(requestId, documentHandle, format);
  });
}
#endif

1. **[P2] Writer manifest retains v32 artifact hashes** — [tools/build_ms_formats_profile.py:62](/home/jiajun/LibreOffice/study_LiteCore/wasm_sdk_probe/tools/build_ms_formats_profile.py:62). The builder copies new artifacts but preserves `editorContract.loaderSha256`, `wasmSha256`, and `workerSha256`. The derived worker already differs: `77be4597…` versus the declared `c394302c…`. Worker init does not enforce these hashes, but artifact validators will reject the profile and manifest-based evidence will identify v32. **Fix:** recompute all three fields from the copied output before writing the manifest.

2. **[P2] Writer’s DOCX save capability can be bypassed through coercion** — [tests/v33-worker-delta.json:18](/home/jiajun/LibreOffice/study_LiteCore/wasm_sdk_probe/tests/v33-worker-delta.json:18). The gate compares `payload.format === "docx"`, whereas save dispatch uses `String(payload.format || "")`. A read-only worker reproduction with `format: ["docx"]` and only `save-odt` granted reached `oxsdk_document_save` with `"docx"`; the ordinary string was correctly refused. **Fix:** reject non-string formats before capability selection, or normalize once and use that same value for gating and dispatch. Apply the same discipline to Calc’s delta.

3. **[P2, validation gap] F3’s predecessor failures do not establish gate-specific causal controls** — [tests/ms_formats_worker_test.cjs:82](/home/jiajun/LibreOffice/study_LiteCore/wasm_sdk_probe/tests/ms_formats_worker_test.cjs:82) and [:107](/home/jiajun/LibreOffice/study_LiteCore/wasm_sdk_probe/tests/ms_formats_worker_test.cjs:107). Both frozen workers fail first because they drop `format`; execution never reaches the refusal assertions. Thus “both predecessors fail” does not fulfill the specified gate-removal red case. **Fix:** retain format forwarding and independently remove each new gate, requiring failure at its corresponding refusal assertion.
